1→"""Direct tests for scan plan reconciliation orchestration.
     2→
     3→Tests exercise the real reconciliation logic with realistic plan and state
     4→data structures, mocking only at I/O boundaries (load_plan, save_plan).
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from types import SimpleNamespace
    11→
    12→import pytest
    13→
    14→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan

    17→    StaleDimensionSyncResult,

    22→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→# Helpers
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    27→    return SimpleNamespace(
    28→        state=state or {},
    29→        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
    30→        config=config or {},
    31→    )
    32→
    33→
    34→def _make_state(
    35→    *,
    36→    issues: dict | None = None,
    37→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    38→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    39→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    40→    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    41→    scan_count: int = 1,
    42→) -> dict:
    43→    """Build a minimal but realistic state dict."""
    44→    state: dict = {
    45→        "issues": issues or {},
    46→        "scan_count": scan_count,
    47→        "dimension_scores": {},
    48→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    49→    }
    50→    if overall_score is not None:
    51→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    52→    if objective_score is not None:
    53→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    54→    if strict_score is not None:
    55→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    56→    if verified_strict_score is not None:
    57→        state["verified_strict_score"] = verified_strict_score
    58→    return state
    59→
    60→
    61→def _make_issue(
    62→    detector: str = "complexity",
    63→    file: str = "src/app.py",
    64→    status: str = "open",
    65→    **extra,
    66→) -> dict:
    67→    return {"detector": detector, "file": file, "status": status, **extra}

   174→class TestApplyPlanReconciliation:
   175→
   176→    def test_supersedes_resolved_issue(self):
   177→        """An issue in queue_order that becomes resolved should be superseded."""
   178→        plan = empty_plan()
   179→        plan["queue_order"] = ["issue-1", "issue-2"]
   180→        plan["overrides"] = {"issue-1": {"issue_id": "issue-1"}}
   181→        state = _make_state(issues={
   182→            "issue-1": _make_issue(status="resolved"),
   183→            "issue-2": _make_issue(status="open"),
   184→        })
   185→        from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
   186→        result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
   187→        assert "issue-1" in result.superseded
   188→        assert "issue-1" in plan["superseded"]
   189→        assert "issue-1" not in plan["queue_order"]
   190→        assert "issue-2" in plan["queue_order"]
   191→
   192→    def test_supersedes_disappeared_issue(self):
   193→        """An issue in queue_order that no longer exists should be superseded."""
   194→        plan = empty_plan()
   195→        plan["queue_order"] = ["gone-id"]
   196→        plan["overrides"] = {"gone-id": {"issue_id": "gone-id"}}
   197→        state = _make_state(issues={})
   198→        from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
   199→        result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
   200→        assert "gone-id" in result.superseded
   201→
   202→    def test_no_changes_when_all_alive(self):
   203→        plan = empty_plan()
   204→        plan["queue_order"] = ["issue-1"]
   205→        plan["overrides"] = {"issue-1": {"issue_id": "issue-1"}}
   206→        state = _make_state(issues={
   207→            "issue-1": _make_issue(status="open"),
   208→        })
   209→        changed = reconcile_mod._apply_plan_reconciliation(
   210→            plan, state, reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   211→        )
   212→        assert changed is False
   213→
   214→    def test_skips_when_no_user_content(self):

   463→class TestReconcilePlanPostScan:
   464→
   465→    def test_saves_when_superseded_issues_detected(self, monkeypatch):
   466→        plan = empty_plan()
   467→        plan["queue_order"] = ["issue-1", "issue-2"]
   468→        plan["overrides"] = {"issue-1": {"issue_id": "issue-1"}}
   469→        state = _make_state(issues={
   470→            "issue-1": _make_issue(status="resolved"),
   471→            "issue-2": _make_issue(status="open"),
   472→        })
   473→
   474→        saved: list[dict] = []
   475→        monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   476→        monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   477→
   478→        reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(_runtime(state=state))
   479→
   480→        assert len(saved) == 1
   481→        assert "issue-1" in saved[0]["superseded"]
   482→        assert "issue-1" not in saved[0]["queue_order"]
   483→        assert "issue-2" in saved[0]["queue_order"]

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    10→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    14→    append_log_entry,
    15→    auto_cluster_issues,
    16→    load_plan,
    17→    reconcile_plan_after_scan,
    18→    save_plan,
    19→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    20→    sync_create_plan_needed,

    28→
    29→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    31→    return bool(
    32→        plan.get("queue_order")
    33→        or plan.get("overrides")
    34→        or plan.get("clusters")
    35→        or plan.get("skipped")
    36→    )
    37→
    38→
    39→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    40→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    41→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    42→        return False
    43→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    44→    if recon.resurfaced:
    45→        print(
    46→            colorize(
    47→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    48→                "cyan",
    49→            )
    50→        )
    51→    return bool(recon.changes)
    52→
    53→
    54→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    55→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    56→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    57→    if sync.injected:
    58→        print(
    59→            colorize(
    60→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
    61→                "cyan",
    62→            )
    63→        )
    64→    return bool(sync.changes)
    65→
    66→
    67→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    68→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
    69→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    70→    if sync.pruned:
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
    74→                "cyan",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    if sync.injected:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
    81→                "cyan",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→    return bool(sync.changes)
    85→
    86→
    87→def _sync_auto_clusters(
    88→    plan: dict[str, object],
    89→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    90→    *,
    91→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    92→    policy=None,
    93→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    94→) -> bool:
    95→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
    96→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
    97→        plan, state,
    98→        target_strict=target_strict,
    99→        policy=policy,
   100→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   101→    ))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   105→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   106→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   107→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   108→        return False
   109→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   110→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   111→        return False
   112→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   113→    if scores.strict is None:
   114→        return False
   115→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   116→        "strict": scores.strict,
   117→        "overall": scores.overall,
   118→        "objective": scores.objective,
   119→        "verified": scores.verified,
   120→    }
   121→    return True
   122→
   123→
   124→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   125→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   126→) -> bool:
   127→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   128→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   129→        return False
   130→
   131→    try:
   132→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   133→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   134→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   135→            score_display_mode,
   136→        )
   137→
   138→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   139→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   140→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   141→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   142→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   143→        return False
   144→    if not queue_empty:
   145→        return False
   146→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   147→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def _subjective_policy_context(
   152→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   153→    plan: dict[str, object],
   154→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   155→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   156→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   157→
   158→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   159→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   160→        runtime.state,
   161→        target_strict=target_strict,
   162→        plan=plan,
   163→    )
   164→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   165→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   166→
   167→
   168→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   169→    plan: dict[str, object],
   170→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   171→) -> bool:
   172→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   173→    if changed:
   174→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   175→    return changed
   176→
   177→
   178→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   179→    plan: dict[str, object],
   180→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   181→    *,
   182→    policy,
   183→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   184→) -> bool:
   185→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   186→        plan,
   187→        state,
   188→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   189→            p,
   190→            s,
   191→            policy=policy,
   192→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   193→        ),
   194→    )
   195→    if changed:
   196→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   197→    return changed
   198→
   199→
   200→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   201→    plan: dict[str, object],
   202→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   203→    *,
   204→    target_strict: float,
   205→    policy,
   206→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   207→) -> bool:
   208→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   209→        plan,
   210→        state,
   211→        target_strict=target_strict,
   212→        policy=policy,
   213→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   214→    )
   215→    if changed:
   216→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   217→    return changed
   218→
   219→
   220→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   221→    plan: dict[str, object],
   222→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   223→) -> bool:
   224→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   225→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   226→        return False
   227→    if triage_sync.injected:
   228→        print(
   229→            colorize(
   230→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   231→                "cyan",
   232→            )
   233→        )
   234→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   235→    return True
   236→
   237→
   238→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   239→    plan: dict[str, object],
   240→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   241→    *,
   242→    policy,
   243→) -> bool:
   244→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   245→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   246→        return False
   247→    append_log_entry(
   248→        plan,
   249→        "sync_communicate_score",
   250→        actor="system",
   251→        detail={"injected": True},
   252→    )
   253→    return True
   254→
   255→
   256→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   257→    plan: dict[str, object],
   258→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   259→    *,
   260→    policy,
   261→) -> bool:
   262→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   263→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   264→        return False
   265→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   266→        print(
   267→            colorize(
   268→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   269→                "cyan",
   270→            )
   271→        )
   272→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   273→    return True
   274→
   275→
   276→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   277→    plan: dict[str, object],
   278→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   279→) -> bool:
   280→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   281→    if seeded:
   282→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   283→        return True
   284→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   285→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   286→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   287→    if cleared:
   288→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   289→    return cleared
   290→
   291→
   292→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   293→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   294→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   295→    try:
   296→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   297→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   298→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   299→        return
   300→    dirty = False
   301→
   302→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   303→        dirty = True
   304→
   305→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   306→        dirty = True
   307→
   308→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   309→        runtime,
   310→        plan,
   311→    )
   312→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   313→        plan,
   314→        runtime.state,
   315→        policy=policy,
   316→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   317→    ):
   318→        dirty = True
   319→
   320→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   321→        plan,
   322→        runtime.state,
   323→        target_strict=target_strict,
   324→        policy=policy,
   325→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   326→    ):
   327→        dirty = True
   328→
   329→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   330→        dirty = True
   331→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   334→        dirty = True
   335→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   336→        dirty = True
   337→
   338→    if dirty:
   339→        try:
   340→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   341→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   342→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   343→

    37→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    38→    build_subjective_items,
    39→    build_triage_stage_items,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    42→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    43→
    44→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    45→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    46→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    # Output control
    59→    count: int | None = 1
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→
    62→    # Scope filtering
    63→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→
   201→
   202→def _gather_workflow_items(
   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   210→    for builder in (
   211→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   212→        build_import_scores_item,
   213→        build_communicate_score_item,
   214→    ):
   215→        item = builder(plan, state)
   216→        if item is not None:
   217→            items.append(item)
   218→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   219→    if plan_item is not None:
   220→        items.append(plan_item)
   221→    return items
   222→
   223→
   224→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   225→
   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   234→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   235→
   236→
   237→def _plan_presort(
   238→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   239→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   240→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   241→
   242→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   243→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   244→    """
   245→    if not plan:
   246→        return set(), []
   247→
   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   249→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   250→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   251→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   252→    items[:] = remaining
   253→    return new_ids, skipped
   254→
   255→
   256→def _plan_postsort(
   257→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   258→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   261→) -> None:
   262→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    if opts.include_skipped:
   267→        items.extend(skipped)
   268→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   269→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   270→    items[:] = focused
   271→
   272→
   273→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   274→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   275→    return any(
   276→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   277→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   278→        for i in items
   279→    )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   283→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   284→    return any(
   285→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   286→        and i.get("initial_review")
   287→        for i in items
   288→    )
   289→
   290→
   291→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   292→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   293→    return (
   294→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   295→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   296→    )
   297→
   298→
   299→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   300→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   301→
   302→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.
   303→
   304→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   305→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   306→    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   307→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   308→    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   309→    """
   310→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   311→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   312→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   313→        return [
   314→            i for i in items
   315→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   316→        ]
   317→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   318→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   319→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   320→
   321→
   322→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   323→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   324→    if not plan:
   325→        return []
   326→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   327→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   328→        return []
   329→    return [{
   330→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   331→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   332→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   333→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   334→        "file": "",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "confidence": "high",
   337→    }]
   338→
   339→
   340→__all__ = [
   341→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   342→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   343→    "QueueContext",
   344→    "WorkQueueResult",
   345→    "build_work_queue",
   346→    "collapse_clusters",
   347→    "group_queue_items",
   348→]
   349→

   190→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   191→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   192→
   193→    Runtime contract is v7-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   194→    """
   195→    defaults = empty_plan()
   196→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   197→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   198→    _upgrade_plan_to_v7(plan)

   201→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   202→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   203→    return {
   204→        name: cluster
   205→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   206→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   212→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   213→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   214→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   215→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   216→
   217→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   218→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   219→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   220→    if overlap:
   221→        raise ValueError(
   222→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   226→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   227→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   228→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   229→            raise ValueError(
   230→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   231→            )
   232→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""

    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→
   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→
   542→
   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   545→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   546→
   547→    injected: bool = False
   548→
   549→    @property
   550→    def changes(self) -> int:
   551→        return int(self.injected)
   552→
   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   654→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",

   103→
   104→def _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   105→    payload: dict[str, object],
   106→    *,
   107→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   108→    override_existing: bool,
   109→) -> None:
   110→    if "display_name" in prompt_meta and (
   111→        override_existing or "display_name" not in payload
   112→    ):
   113→        payload["display_name"] = prompt_meta["display_name"]
   114→    if "weight" in prompt_meta and (override_existing or "weight" not in payload):
   115→        payload["weight"] = prompt_meta["weight"]
   116→    if "reset_on_scan" in prompt_meta and (
   117→        override_existing or "reset_on_scan" not in payload
   118→    ):
   119→        payload["reset_on_scan"] = prompt_meta["reset_on_scan"]
   120→
   121→
   122→def _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   123→    payload: dict[str, object],
   124→    *,
   125→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   126→    override_existing: bool,
   127→    default_enabled: bool,
   128→) -> None:
   129→    if default_enabled:
   130→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = True
   131→    if "enabled_by_default" not in prompt_meta:
   132→        return
   133→    prompt_enabled = bool(prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"])
   134→    if override_existing:
   135→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = prompt_enabled
   136→        return
   137→    payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(
   138→        payload.get("enabled_by_default", False) or prompt_enabled
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions: list[str]) -> set[str]:
   143→    return {
   144→        _normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   145→        for dim in dimensions
   146→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   147→    }
   148→
   149→
   150→def _merge_dimension_meta(
   151→    target: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   152→    *,
   153→    dimensions: list[str],
   154→    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   155→    override_existing: bool = False,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    defaults = _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions)
   158→
   159→    for raw_dim, entry in prompts.items():
   160→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw_dim)
   161→        if not dim:
   162→            continue
   163→
   164→        payload = target.setdefault(dim, {})
   165→        prompt_meta = extract_prompt_meta(entry)
   166→        _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   167→            payload,
   168→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   169→            override_existing=override_existing,
   170→        )
   171→        _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   172→            payload,
   173→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   174→            override_existing=override_existing,
   175→            default_enabled=dim in defaults,
   176→        )
   177→
   178→
   179→def _default_available_languages() -> list[str]:

   198→class _SubjectiveProviderState:

   202→        )
   203→        self.load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   204→            [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   205→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload
   206→        self.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   207→            [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   208→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang
   209→
   210→
   211→_PROVIDER_STATE = _SubjectiveProviderState()

   214→def _available_languages() -> list[str]:

   216→
   217→

   219→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_provider()
   220→
   221→
   222→def _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(
   223→    lang_name: str,
   224→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   225→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider(lang_name)
   226→
   227→
   228→def _clear_subjective_dimension_caches() -> None:
   229→    default_dimension_keys.cache_clear()
   230→    default_dimension_keys_for_lang.cache_clear()
   231→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata.cache_clear()
   232→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang.cache_clear()
   233→
   234→
   235→def configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   236→    *,
   237→    available_languages_provider: Callable[[], list[str]] | None = None,
   238→    load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   239→        [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   240→    ]
   241→    | None = None,
   242→    load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   243→        [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   244→    ]
   245→    | None = None,
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Configure metadata providers for subjective-dimension lookups."""
   248→    state = _PROVIDER_STATE
   249→
   250→    changed = False
   251→    if (
   252→        available_languages_provider is not None
   253→        and available_languages_provider is not state.available_languages_provider
   254→    ):
   255→        state.available_languages_provider = available_languages_provider
   256→        changed = True
   257→    if (
   258→        load_dimensions_payload_provider is not None
   259→        and load_dimensions_payload_provider
   260→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_provider
   261→    ):
   262→        state.load_dimensions_payload_provider = load_dimensions_payload_provider
   263→        changed = True
   264→    if (
   265→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider is not None
   266→        and load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   267→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   268→    ):
   269→        state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider = (
   270→            load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   271→        )
   272→        changed = True
   273→
   274→    if changed:
   275→        _clear_subjective_dimension_caches()
   276→
   277→
   278→def reset_subjective_dimension_providers() -> None:
   279→    """Reset metadata providers to built-in defaults."""
   280→    configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   281→        available_languages_provider=_default_available_languages,
   282→        load_dimensions_payload_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload,
   283→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang,
   284→    )
   285→
   286→
   287→def _normalize_dimension_list(values: list[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   288→    normalized: list[str] = []
   289→    for raw in values:
   290→        if not isinstance(raw, str):
   291→            continue
   292→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw)
   293→        if not dim or dim in normalized:
   294→            continue
   295→        normalized.append(dim)
   296→    return tuple(normalized)
   297→
   298→
   299→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   300→def default_dimension_keys() -> tuple[str, ...]:
   301→    """Return canonical default subjective dimension keys."""
   302→    try:
   303→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   304→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   305→        logger.debug("Failed to load default subjective dimensions: %s", exc)
   306→        return tuple(_LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.keys())
   307→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   308→
   309→
   310→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   311→def default_dimension_keys_for_lang(lang_name: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   312→    """Return default subjective dimension keys for a specific language."""
   313→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   314→    if normalized is None:
   315→        return default_dimension_keys()
   316→    try:
   317→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(normalized)
   318→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   319→        logger.debug(
   320→            "Failed to load subjective dimensions for lang %s: %s",
   321→            normalized,
   322→            exc,
   323→        )
   324→        return default_dimension_keys()
   325→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   326→
   327→
   328→def _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(
   329→    *,
   330→    lang_name: str | None,
   331→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   332→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   333→
   334→    try:
   335→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   336→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   337→        logger.debug("Failed to load shared subjective dimension payload: %s", exc)
   338→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts = [], {}
   339→    _merge_dimension_meta(out, dimensions=shared_defaults, prompts=shared_prompts)
   340→
   341→    langs = (
   342→        [lang_name]
   343→        if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip()
   344→        else _available_languages()
   345→    )
   346→    for name in langs:
   347→        try:
   348→            lang_defaults, lang_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(name)
   349→            _merge_dimension_meta(
   350→                out,
   351→                dimensions=lang_defaults,
   352→                prompts=lang_prompts,
   353→                override_existing=bool(lang_name),
   354→            )
   355→        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   356→            logger.debug("Failed to load dimensions for lang %s: %s", name, exc)
   357→            continue
   358→
   359→    for dim, payload in out.items():
   360→        payload.setdefault(
   361→            "display_name",
   362→            _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, _title_display_name(dim)),
   363→        )
   364→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   365→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   366→        if dim in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES:
   367→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   368→        else:
   369→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   370→
   371→    # Preserve legacy dimensions even if a payload temporarily drops one.
   372→    for dim, display in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   373→        payload = out.setdefault(dim, {})
   374→        payload.setdefault("display_name", display)
   375→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   376→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", True)
   377→        payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   378→
   379→    return out
   380→
   381→
   382→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   383→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   384→    """Return merged metadata across all known dimensions/languages."""
   385→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=None)
   386→
   387→
   388→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   389→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(
   390→    lang_name: str | None,
   391→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   392→    """Return merged metadata for one language (with language overrides)."""
   393→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   394→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=normalized)
   395→
   396→
   397→def _metadata_registry(lang_name: str | None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   398→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   399→    if normalized is None:
   400→        return load_subjective_dimension_metadata()
   401→    return load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(normalized)
   402→
   403→
   404→def get_dimension_metadata(
   405→    dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None
   406→) -> dict[str, object]:
   407→    """Return metadata for one dimension key (with sane defaults)."""
   408→    dim = _normalize_dimension_name(dimension_name)
   409→    all_meta = _metadata_registry(lang_name)
   410→    payload = dict(all_meta.get(dim, {}))
   411→
   412→    payload.setdefault("display_name", _title_display_name(dim))
   413→    payload.setdefault("weight", 1.0)
   414→    payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   415→    payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   416→    return payload
   417→
   418→
   419→def dimension_display_name(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> str:
   420→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   421→    return str(meta.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dimension_name)))
   422→
   423→
   424→def dimension_weight(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> float:
   425→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   426→    raw = meta.get("weight", 1.0)
   427→    if is_numeric(raw):
   428→        return max(0.0, float(raw))
   429→    return 1.0
   430→
   431→
   432→def default_display_names_map(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
   433→    """Display-name map for default subjective dimensions."""
   434→    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   435→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   436→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   437→            continue
   438→        out[dim] = str(payload.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dim)))
   439→    return out
   440→
   441→
   442→def resettable_default_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   443→    """Default subjective dimensions that should be reset by scan reset."""
   444→    out = []
   445→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   446→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   447→            continue
   448→        if not bool(payload.get("reset_on_scan", True)):
   449→            continue
   450→        out.append(dim)
   451→    return tuple(sorted(set(out)))
   452→
   453→
   454→__all__ = [
   455→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   456→    "configure_subjective_dimension_providers",
   457→    "default_dimension_keys",
   458→    "default_dimension_keys_for_lang",
   459→    "default_display_names_map",
   460→    "dimension_display_name",
   461→    "dimension_weight",
   462→    "get_dimension_metadata",
   463→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata",
   464→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang",
   465→    "reset_subjective_dimension_providers",
   466→    "resettable_default_dimensions",
   467→]
   468→

1→"""Tests for shared queue selection in desloppify.work_queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import build_work_queue as _build_work_queue
     7→
     8→
     9→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    10→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    11→
    12→
    13→def _issue(
    14→    fid: str,
    15→    *,
    16→    detector: str = "smells",
    17→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    18→    tier: int = 3,
    19→    confidence: str = "medium",
    20→    status: str = "open",
    21→    detail: dict | None = None,
    22→) -> dict:
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": fid,
    25→        "detector": detector,
    26→        "file": file,
    27→        "tier": tier,
    28→        "confidence": confidence,
    29→        "summary": fid,
    30→        "status": status,
    31→        "detail": detail or {},
    32→    }
    33→
    34→
    35→def _state(issues: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    36→    return {
    37→        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
    38→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    39→    }
    40→
    41→
    42→def test_review_issue_uses_natural_tier():
    43→    review = _issue(
    44→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    45→        detector="review",
    46→        tier=2,
    47→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    48→    )
    49→    mechanical = _issue("smells::src/a.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
    50→    state = _state(
    51→        [review, mechanical],
    52→        dimension_scores={
    53→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "failing": 1}
    54→        },
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    58→    by_id = {item["id"]: item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "issue"}
    59→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in by_id
    60→    assert "smells::src/a.py::x" in by_id

    88→def test_review_items_sort_by_issue_weight_within_tier():

    93→        confidence="high",

    96→    holistic = _issue(
    97→        "review::src/a.py::logic",
    98→        detector="review",
    99→        tier=2,
   100→        confidence="high",
   101→        detail={"dimension": "logic_clarity", "holistic": True},
   102→    )
   103→    state = _state(

   378→# ── Subjective threshold clamping ─────────────────────────
   379→
   380→
   381→def test_subjective_threshold_clamped_to_valid_range():
   382→    """Threshold values outside [0, 100] are clamped, not rejected."""
   383→    state = _state(
   384→        [],
   385→        dimension_scores={
   386→            "Naming quality": {"score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "failing": 1, "stale": True},
   387→        },
   388→    )
   389→    # threshold=-10 clamps to 0.0 -> score 50 >= 0 -> item excluded
   390→    queue = build_work_queue(
   391→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=-10
   392→    )
   393→    subj_items = [item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   394→    assert subj_items == []
   395→
   396→    # threshold=200 clamps to 100.0 -> score 50 < 100 -> item included
   397→    queue2 = build_work_queue(
   398→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=200
   399→    )
   400→    subj_items2 = [item for item in queue2["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   401→    assert len(subj_items2) >= 1
   402→

   533→def test_stale_subjective_gated_when_objective_backlog_exists():
   534→    """Stale subjective items are suppressed while objective backlog exists."""
   535→    objective_issues = [
   536→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   537→        for c in "abcd"
   538→    ]
   539→    state = _state(
   540→        objective_issues,
   541→        dimension_scores={
   542→            "Naming quality": {
   543→                "score": 70.0,
   544→                "strict": 70.0,
   545→                "failing": 1,
   546→                "detectors": {
   547→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   548→                },
   549→            },
   550→        },
   551→    )
   552→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   553→        "naming_quality": {
   554→            "score": 70.0,
   555→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   556→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   557→        }
   558→    }
   559→
   560→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   561→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   562→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   563→
   564→    # Stale subjective items are gated when objective backlog > 0
   565→    assert len(subj_ids) == 0
   566→    assert len(ids) == 4  # 4 objective only
   567→

   599→
   600→def test_unassessed_subjective_visible_with_objective_backlog():
   601→    """When initial reviews exist, only they are shown — objective items hidden.
   602→
   603→    Initial reviews are the first phase: scan → review → score → plan.
   604→    Objective work becomes visible only after initial reviews are done.
   605→    """
   606→    objective_issues = [
   607→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   608→        for c in "abcd"
   609→    ]
   610→    state = _state(
   611→        objective_issues,
   612→        dimension_scores={
   613→            "Naming quality": {
   614→                "score": 0.0,
   615→                "strict": 0.0,
   616→                "failing": 0,
   617→                "detectors": {
   618→                    "subjective_assessment": {
   619→                        "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
   620→                        "placeholder": True,
   621→                    },
   622→                },
   623→            },
   624→        },
   625→    )
   626→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   627→        "naming_quality": {
   628→            "score": 0.0,
   629→            "placeholder": True,
   630→        }
   631→    }
   632→
   633→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   634→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   635→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   636→
   637→    # Initial reviews gate: only subjective items visible, objective hidden
   638→    assert len(subj_ids) == 1
   639→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in ids
   640→    assert len(ids) == 1  # only initial review — objective items hidden
   641→
