   643→# ── Impact-based ordering ──────────────────────────────────
   644→
   645→
   646→def test_high_impact_subjective_before_low_impact_mechanical():
   647→    """A subjective dimension with high headroom sorts before a mechanical
   648→    issue in a near-perfect dimension."""
   649→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   650→
   651→    subj_item = {
   652→        "id": "subjective::naming",
   653→        "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   654→        "detail": {"dimension_name": "Naming quality", "strict_score": 43.0},
   655→        "confidence": "medium",
   656→    }
   657→    mech_item = {
   658→        "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   659→        "kind": "issue",
   660→        "detector": "smells",
   661→        "confidence": "high",
   662→        "detail": {"count": 1},
   663→    }
   664→
   665→    dimension_scores = {
   666→        "Naming quality": {
   667→            "score": 43.0,
   668→            "strict": 43.0,
   669→            "failing": 3,
   670→            "detectors": {
   671→                "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   672→            },
   673→        },
   674→        "Code quality": {
   675→            "score": 97.0,
   676→            "strict": 97.0,
   677→            "checks": 200,
   678→            "failing": 1,
   679→            "detectors": {
   680→                "smells": {"weighted_failures": 1, "potential": 200},
   681→            },
   682→        },
   683→    }
   684→
   685→    enrich_with_impact([subj_item, mech_item], dimension_scores)
   686→
   687→    # Naming quality has much more headroom (57) than Code quality (3)
   688→    assert subj_item["estimated_impact"] > mech_item["estimated_impact"]
   689→    assert item_sort_key(subj_item) < item_sort_key(mech_item)
   690→
   691→
   692→def test_impact_fallback_when_no_dimension_scores():
   693→    """When no dimension_scores exist, all items get impact 0.0 and
   694→    tiebreakers provide default ordering."""
   695→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   696→
   697→    items = [
   698→        {
   699→            "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   700→            "kind": "issue",
   701→            "detector": "smells",
   702→            "confidence": "high",
   703→            "detail": {"count": 5},
   704→        },
   705→        {
   706→            "id": "smells::src/b.py::y",
   707→            "kind": "issue",
   708→            "detector": "smells",
   709→            "confidence": "medium",
   710→            "detail": {"count": 2},
   711→        },
   712→    ]
   713→
   714→    enrich_with_impact(items, {})
   715→
   716→    assert all(item["estimated_impact"] == 0.0 for item in items)
   717→    # High confidence sorts before medium when impact is equal
   718→    assert item_sort_key(items[0]) < item_sort_key(items[1])

   842→# ── Cluster collapse ─────────────────────────────────────
   843→
   844→
   845→def test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order():
   846→    """Cluster meta-item appears at position of first member, not re-sorted."""
   847→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
   848→
   849→    plan: dict = {
   850→        "queue_order": [],
   851→        "skipped": {},
   852→        "overrides": {},
   853→        "clusters": {},
   854→        "active_cluster": None,
   855→    }
   856→    plan["clusters"]["auto/unused"] = {
   857→        "name": "auto/unused",
   858→        "auto": True,
   859→        "cluster_key": "auto::unused",
   860→        "issue_ids": ["u1", "u2"],
   861→        "description": "Remove 2 unused issues",
   862→        "action": "desloppify autofix unused-imports --dry-run",
   863→        "user_modified": False,
   864→    }
   865→
   866→    # Place a non-cluster item first, then the two cluster members
   867→    items = [
   868→        {"id": "other", "kind": "issue", "detector": "structural",
   869→         "confidence": "medium", "detail": {}},
   870→        {"id": "u1", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
   871→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   872→        {"id": "u2", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
   873→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   874→    ]
   875→
   876→    result = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   877→    # Non-cluster item stays at position 0
   878→    assert result[0]["id"] == "other"
   879→    # Cluster meta-item appears at position 1 (where first member was)
   880→    assert result[1]["kind"] == "cluster"
   881→    assert result[1]["id"] == "auto/unused"
   882→    assert len(result) == 2  # other + cluster
   883→

   888→def test_plan_ordered_stale_subjective_gated_with_objective_backlog():
   889→    """Stale subjective items are gated by lifecycle filter while objective
   890→    issues exist, even when the plan includes them in queue_order.
   891→    """
   892→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   893→

   903→                "strict": 70.0,
   904→                "failing": 1,

   914→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   915→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   916→        }
   917→    }
   918→
   919→    # Without plan: stale subjective item is gated
   920→    queue_no_plan = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   921→    subj_no_plan = [
   922→        i["id"] for i in queue_no_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   923→    ]
   924→    assert len(subj_no_plan) == 0
   925→
   926→    # With plan that includes the stale dim in queue_order: still gated
   927→    plan = empty_plan()
   928→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   929→        "subjective::naming_quality",

   935→    queue_with_plan = build_work_queue(

   938→    subj_with_plan = [
   939→        i["id"] for i in queue_with_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   940→    ]
   941→    # Stale subjective items gated by lifecycle filter even with plan ordering
   942→    assert len(subj_with_plan) == 0

   948→def test_skipped_objective_items_dont_block_subjective():
   949→    """Plan-skipped objective items are removed before lifecycle filter,
   950→    so they don't block subjective reassessment items.
   951→    """
   952→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   953→
   954→    objective_issues = [

   956→        for c in "abcd"

   959→        objective_issues,
   960→        dimension_scores={
   961→            "Naming quality": {
   962→                "score": 70.0,
   963→                "strict": 70.0,
   964→                "failing": 1,
   965→                "detectors": {
   966→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   967→                },
   968→            },
   969→        },
   970→    )
   971→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   972→        "naming_quality": {
   973→            "score": 70.0,
   974→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   975→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   976→        }
   977→    }
   978→
   979→    # Skip ALL objective issues in the plan
   980→    plan = empty_plan()
   981→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   982→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   983→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   984→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   985→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   986→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   987→    ]
   988→    plan["skipped"] = {
   989→        "smells::src/a.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   990→        "smells::src/b.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   991→        "smells::src/c.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   992→        "smells::src/d.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   993→    }
   994→
   995→    queue = build_work_queue(
   996→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   997→    )
   998→    subj_ids = [
   999→        i["id"] for i in queue["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
  1000→    ]
  1001→    # All objective items skipped → lifecycle filter sees no objective work
  1002→    # → stale subjective item surfaces
  1003→    assert len(subj_ids) == 1
  1004→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in subj_ids

  1010→def test_wontfixed_issues_excluded_from_queue():
  1011→    """Issues with status 'wontfix' never appear in the default queue.
  1012→
  1013→    Regression test for #193: wontfixed issues were leaking into the
  1014→    auto-generated queue because filtering was spread across multiple
  1015→    modules and easy to miss.
  1016→    """
  1017→    state = _state(
  1018→        [
  1019→            _issue("a", status="open"),
  1020→            _issue("b", status="wontfix"),

  1022→            _issue("d", status="open"),
  1023→        ]
  1024→    )
  1025→
  1026→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)

  1028→    assert "a" in ids
  1029→    assert "d" in ids

  1031→    assert "c" not in ids  # fixed excluded
  1032→

Excellent! I have all the files. Let me now provide you with a comprehensive summary of the key patterns, signatures, and data structures needed for your lifecycle integration test suite.

## Summary: Key Patterns, Function Signatures, and Data Structures

### 1. **Test Patterns & Mocking Approach** (`test_plan_reconcile.py`)

#### `_runtime()` Helper
```python
def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(
        state=state or {},
        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
        config=config or {},
    )
```
- Used to mock the CLI runtime object passed to commands
- Must include: `state` (dict), `state_path` (Path), `config` (dict)

#### `_make_state()` Helper
```python
def _make_state(
    *,
    issues: dict | None = None,
    overall_score: float | None = None,
    objective_score: float | None = None,
    strict_score: float | None = None,
    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    scan_count: int = 1,
) -> dict
```
- Creates minimal but realistic state dicts
- Keys: `"issues"`, `"scan_count"`, `"dimension_scores"`, `"subjective_assessments"`
- Optional scoring keys: `"overall_score"`, `"objective_score"`, `"strict_score"`, `"verified_strict_score"`

#### `_make_issue()` Helper
```python
def _make_issue(
    detector: str = "complexity",
    file: str = "src/app.py",
    status: str = "open",
    **extra,
) -> dict
```
- Creates issue dicts with: `"detector"`, `"file"`, `"status"`, plus any extra fields
- Status values: `"open"`, `"resolved"`, `"fixed"`, `"wontfix"`

#### Mocking Pattern
```python
monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
```
- Monkeypatch plan I/O at the module level
- Capture saved plans into a list for assertions

---

### 2. **Core Function Signatures**

#### `reconcile_plan_post_scan()`
**From:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`
```python
def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
```
- **Input:** `runtime` with `.state`, `.state_path`, `.config`
- **Behavior:** Orchestrates multiple reconciliation steps; saves plan only if dirty
- **Orchestration chain:**
  1. `_apply_plan_reconciliation()` — supersede resolved/disappeared issues
  2. `_sync_unscored_and_log()` — inject unscored dimensions
  3. `_sync_stale_and_log()` — inject/prune stale dimensions
  4. `_sync_auto_clusters_and_log()` — regenerate task clusters
  5. `_sync_communicate_score_and_log()` — inject communicate-score workflow
  6. `_sync_create_plan_and_log()` — inject create-plan workflow
  7. `_sync_triage_and_log()` — inject triage stages if reviews changed
  8. `_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log()` — seed or clear frozen scores

#### `build_work_queue()`
**From:** `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`
```python
def build_work_queue(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
) -> WorkQueueResult:
```
- **Returns:** TypedDict with `items`, `total`, `grouped`, `new_ids`
- **Pipeline:**
  1. **Gather** — build issue items + subjective dimensions + workflow stages
  2. **Score** — enrich with impact, apply impact floor
  3. **Presort** — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
  4. **Lifecycle filter** — `_apply_lifecycle_filter()` enforces phase ordering
  5. **Sort** — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
  6. **Limit** — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata

#### `_apply_lifecycle_filter()`
**From:** `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`
```python
def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules."""
```
- **Phase 1:** If initial reviews exist → return only `subjective_dimension` items with `initial_review=True`
- **Phase 2:** If objective work remains → filter out `endgame_only` items (stale re-reviews)
- **Phase 3:** Objective drained → everything visible
- **Key helpers:**
  - `_has_initial_reviews()` — checks for unscored subjective items
  - `_has_objective_items()` — checks for non-subjective mechanical work
  - `_is_endgame_only()` — checks kind and `initial_review` flag

### 3. **Plan Schema & Empty Plan Structure**

#### `empty_plan()` Returns
**From:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`
```python
PlanModel = TypedDict with keys:
  version: Required[int]              # PLAN_VERSION = 7
  created: Required[str]              # ISO timestamp
  updated: Required[str]              # ISO timestamp
  queue_order: list[str]              # IDs in user-chosen order
  deferred: list[str]                 # kept empty for migration compat
  skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]       # user-deferred/permanent/false-positive
  active_cluster: str | None
  overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]  # user descriptions/notes
  clusters: dict[str, Cluster]        # auto and user-created task groups
  superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]  # resolved/disappeared issues
  promoted_ids: list[str]             # IDs user explicitly positioned
  plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores  # frozen score snapshot (dict)
  execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]  # audit trail
  epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta    # triage checkpoint state
  commit_log: list[CommitRecord]      # git commit history
  uncommitted_issues: list[str]       # issues mentioned in WIP commits
  commit_tracking_branch: str | None
```

#### Key TypedDicts
```python
class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    issue_id: Required[str]
    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    reason: str | None
    note: str | None
    attestation: str | None
    created_at: str
    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped

class PlanStartScores(TypedDict, total=False):
    strict: float
    overall: float
    objective: float
    verified: float
    reset: bool  # sentinel to force reseed
```

### 4. **Stale Dimensions & Sync Constants**

#### Result Dataclasses
**From:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`
```python
@dataclass
class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)

@dataclass
class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
```

#### ID Prefix Constants
```python
SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    "triage::observe",
    "triage::reflect",
    "triage::organize",
    "triage::commit",
)
WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
```

#### Sync Function Signatures
```python
def sync_unscored_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """Prepend unscored IDs unconditionally (onboarding priority)."""

def sync_stale_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    """Inject stale/under-target dimensions when no objective backlog or cycle just completed."""

def sync_triage_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> TriageSyncResult:
    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs when review issues change."""

def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes. Empty string if no open review issues."""
```

#### ID Discovery Functions
```python
def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """subjective::<slug> IDs that are placeholder (never scored)."""

def current_under_target_ids(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
) -> set[str]:
    """subjective::<slug> IDs under target but not stale/unscored."""

def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """subjective::<slug> IDs that are currently stale."""

def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """Set of open issue IDs with detector 'review' or 'concerns'."""
```

### 6. **QueueBuildOptions & WorkQueueItem**

#### QueueBuildOptions Dataclass
**From:** `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    # Output control
    count: int | None = 1
    explain: bool = False
    
    # Scope filtering
    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE  # sentinel
    scope: str | None = None
    status: str = "open"  # "open", "fixed", "all"
    chronic: bool = False
    
    # Subjective gating
    include_subjective: bool = True
    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    
    # Plan integration
    plan: dict | None = None
    include_skipped: bool = False
    cluster: str | None = None
    
    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    context: QueueContext | None = None
```

#### WorkQueueResult TypedDict
```python
class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    items: list[WorkQueueItem]  # sorted queue items
    total: int                  # count before limiting
    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    new_ids: set[str]          # IDs new since last triage
```

#### WorkQueueItem Fields
From test patterns in `work_queue_cases.py`:
```python
WorkQueueItem fields:
  id: str                    # unique identifier
  kind: str                  # "issue", "subjective_dimension", "workflow_action", "workflow_stage", "cluster"
  detector: str              # detector name
  tier: int                  # 1-4 priority
  confidence: str            # "high", "medium", "low"
  summary: str               # human-readable description
  status: str                # "open", "fixed", etc.
  file: str                  # file path
  detail: dict               # detector-specific metadata
  is_review: bool (optional)
  is_subjective: bool (optional)
  initial_review: bool (optional, for subjective items)
  estimated_impact: float (optional)
  primary_command: str (optional)
  explain: dict (optional, if explain=True)
```

### 7. **Test Patterns from `work_queue_cases.py`**

#### Helper Functions
```python
def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    """Wrapper that wraps kwargs in QueueBuildOptions."""
    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))

def _issue(
    fid: str,
    *,
    detector: str = "smells",
    file: str = "src/a.py",
    tier: int = 3,
    confidence: str = "medium",
    status: str = "open",
    detail: dict | None = None,
) -> dict:
    """Factory for creating test issues."""

def _state(issues: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    """Factory for creating test state."""
    return {
        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    }
```

#### State Structure
```python
state = {
    "issues": {
        "issue-id": {
            "id": str,
            "detector": str,
            "file": str,
            "tier": int,
            "confidence": str,
            "summary": str,
            "status": str,
            "detail": dict,
        }
    },
    "dimension_scores": {
        "Display Name": {
            "score": float,
            "strict": float,
            "failing": int,
            "stale": bool (optional),
            "checks": int (optional),
            "detectors": dict (optional),
        }
    },
    "subjective_assessments": {
        "dimension_key": {
            "score": float,
            "placeholder": bool (optional),
            "needs_review_refresh": bool (optional),
            "stale_since": str (ISO timestamp, optional),
        }
    },
}
```

---

### 8. **Key Constants & Behavior Rules**

#### Lifecycle Filter Phases
1. **Phase 1 (Initial Reviews):** If `unscored_ids` exist → only show `subjective_dimension` items with `initial_review=True`
2. **Phase 2 (Objective Work):** If objective items remain → hide `endgame_only` items (stale re-reviews)
3. **Phase 3 (Endgame):** All items visible

#### Non-Objective Detectors
```python
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS = {
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_assessment", "subjective_review"
}
```

#### Score Keys in `plan_start_scores`
- `"strict"` — subjective (strict) score
- `"overall"` — blended overall score
- `"objective"` — mechanical work only
- `"verified"` — verified strict (for attestation mode)

#### Scoring Constants
```python
DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 85.0  # from base/config.py
```

---

### 9. **Key Assertions Patterns**

From test examples:
```python
# Superseding
assert "issue-1" in plan["superseded"]
assert "issue-1" not in plan["queue_order"]

# Syncing dimensions
assert "subjective::naming" in plan["queue_order"]
assert "subjective::naming" in result.injected

# Scoring
assert plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"] == 85.0

# Execution log
log_actions = [e["action"] for e in plan["execution_log"]]
assert "reconcile" in log_actions
assert "seed_start_scores" in log_actions

# Queue building
ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
assert "issue-1" in ids

# Lifecycle filtering
subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
assert len(subj_ids) == 0  # blocked when objective work remains
```

1→"""Pattern → issue-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_issues
     7→
     8→
     9→def _append_unique(fid: str, seen: set[str], result: list[str]) -> None:
    10→    if fid in seen:
    11→        return
    12→    seen.add(fid)
    13→    result.append(fid)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _collect_plan_ids(plan: PlanModel | None) -> set[str]:
    17→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    18→    if plan is None:
    19→        return plan_ids
    20→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    21→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    22→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    23→        plan_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    24→    return plan_ids
    25→
    26→
    27→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    28→    state: StateModel,
    29→    patterns: list[str],
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    32→    status_filter: str = "open",
    33→) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of issue IDs.
    35→
    36→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    37→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    38→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["issues"]``.
    39→    """
    40→    seen: set[str] = set()
    41→    result: list[str] = []
    42→    plan_ids = _collect_plan_ids(plan)
    43→
    44→    for pattern in patterns:
    45→        matches = match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    46→        if matches:
    47→            for issue in matches:
    48→                _append_unique(issue["id"], seen, result)
    49→            continue
    50→        if pattern in plan_ids:
    51→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state issues
    52→            _append_unique(pattern, seen, result)
    53→            continue
    54→        if plan is not None and pattern in plan.get("clusters", {}):
    55→            # Cluster name → expand to member IDs
    56→            for fid in plan["clusters"][pattern].get("issue_ids", []):
    57→                _append_unique(fid, seen, result)
    58→    return result
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    62→

1→"""Lifecycle and reset mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
    16→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
    19→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    20→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
    21→
    22→
    23→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    24→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
    25→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    26→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
    27→
    28→
    29→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    30→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
    31→
    32→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
    33→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
    34→    """
    35→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
    36→    plan.clear()
    37→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
    38→        plan[k] = v
    39→    plan["created"] = created
    40→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
    41→
    42→
    43→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    44→    """Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.
    45→
    46→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
    47→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
    48→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
    49→    """
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    51→    found = 0
    52→
    53→    purge_set = set(issue_ids)
    54→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, purge_set)
    55→
    56→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    57→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    58→    for fid in issue_ids:
    59→        was_present = False
    60→        if fid in order:
    61→            order.remove(fid)
    62→            was_present = True
    63→        if fid in skipped:
    64→            skipped.pop(fid)
    65→            was_present = True
    66→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    67→            ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if fid in ids:
    69→                ids.remove(fid)
    70→                was_present = True
    71→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
    72→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    73→            override["cluster"] = None
    74→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
    75→        if was_present:
    76→            found += 1
    77→
    78→    return found
    79→
    80→
    81→__all__ = ["clear_focus", "purge_ids", "reset_plan", "set_focus"]
    82→

1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_issues",
    10→    "resolve_issues",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→from desloppify.engine._state import _recompute_stats
    24→
    25→
    26→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    27→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    28→
    29→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    30→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    31→    """
    32→    if is_numeric(value):
    33→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    34→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    35→        raw = value.get("score")
    36→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    37→            return None
    38→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    39→    return None
    40→
    41→
    42→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    43→    state: StateModel,
    44→    *,
    45→    status: str,
    46→    resolved_issues: list[dict],
    47→    now: str,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review issues are resolved.
    50→
    51→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    52→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    53→    for a re-review.
    54→    """
    55→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    56→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    60→    for issue in resolved_issues:
    61→        if issue.get("detector") != "review":
    62→            continue
    63→        dimension = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    64→        if dimension:
    65→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    66→
    67→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    68→        if dimension not in assessments:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    72→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    73→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    74→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
    75→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    76→        else:
    77→            assessments[dimension] = {
    78→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    79→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    80→                "refresh_reason": f"review_issue_{status}",
    81→                "stale_since": now,
    82→            }
    83→
    84→
    85→def match_issues(
    86→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    87→) -> list[dict]:
    88→    """Return issues matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    89→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    90→    return [
    91→        issue
    92→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
    93→        if not issue.get("suppressed")
    94→        if (status_filter == "all" or issue["status"] == status_filter)
    95→        and _matches_pattern(issue_id, issue, pattern)
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→
    99→def resolve_issues(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    pattern: str,
   102→    status: str,
   103→    note: str | None = None,
   104→    attestation: str | None = None,
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Set issue status for matches and return affected issue IDs."""
   107→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   108→    now = utc_now()
   109→    resolved: list[str] = []
   110→    resolved_issues: list[dict] = []
   111→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   112→    for issue in match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   113→        previous_status = str(issue.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   114→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   115→            continue
   116→
   117→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   118→        if status == "wontfix":
   119→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   120→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   121→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   122→                "captured_at": now,
   123→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   124→                "tier": issue.get("tier"),
   125→                "confidence": issue.get("confidence"),
   126→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(issue.get("detail", {})),
   127→            }
   128→        if status == "open":
   129→            issue["reopen_count"] = int(issue.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   130→            issue.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   131→            issue.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   132→            previous_note = issue.get("note")
   133→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   135→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   136→            reopen_attestation = {
   137→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   138→                "text": attestation or note,
   139→                "attested_at": now,
   140→                "scan_verified": False,
   141→            }
   142→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   143→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   144→
   145→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   146→            "status": status,
   147→            "note": note,
   148→            "resolved_at": now,
   149→            "suppressed": False,
   150→            "suppressed_at": None,
   151→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   152→            "resolution_attestation": {
   153→                "kind": "manual",
   154→                "text": attestation,
   155→                "attested_at": now,
   156→                "scan_verified": False,
   157→            },
   158→        }
   159→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   160→        issue.update(updates)
   161→        resolved.append(issue["id"])
   162→        resolved_issues.append(issue)
   163→
   164→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   165→        state,
   166→        status=status,
   167→        resolved_issues=resolved_issues,
   168→        now=now,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   172→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   173→    return resolved
   174→
```

1→"""Skip/unskip mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import _remove_id_from_lists
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_needs_state_reopen
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    10→
    11→
    12→def skip_items(
    13→    plan: PlanModel,
    14→    issue_ids: list[str],
    15→    *,
    16→    kind: str = "temporary",
    17→    reason: str | None = None,
    18→    note: str | None = None,
    19→    attestation: str | None = None,
    20→    review_after: int | None = None,
    21→    scan_count: int = 0,
    22→) -> int:
    23→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    24→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    25→    now = utc_now()
    26→    count = 0
    27→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    28→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    29→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    30→    for fid in issue_ids:
    31→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    32→        skipped[fid] = {
    33→            "issue_id": fid,
    34→            "kind": kind,
    35→            "reason": reason,
    36→            "note": note,
    37→            "attestation": attestation,
    38→            "created_at": now,
    39→            "review_after": review_after,
    40→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    41→        }
    42→        count += 1
    43→    return count
    44→
    45→
    46→def unskip_items(
    47→    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]
    48→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    49→    """Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
    50→
    51→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
    52→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
    53→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
    54→    """
    55→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    56→    count = 0
    57→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
    58→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    59→    for fid in issue_ids:
    60→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
    61→        if entry is not None:
    62→            if skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(str(entry.get("kind", ""))):
    63→                need_reopen.append(fid)
    64→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
    65→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
    66→            count += 1
    67→    return count, need_reopen
    68→
    69→
    70→def resurface_stale_skips(
    71→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
    72→) -> list[str]:
    73→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
    74→
    75→    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.
    76→    """
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    79→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
    80→    for fid in list(skipped):
    81→        entry = skipped[fid]
    82→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
    83→            continue
    84→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
    85→        if review_after is None:
    86→            continue
    87→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
    88→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
    89→            skipped.pop(fid)
    90→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
    91→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
    92→            resurfaced.append(fid)
    93→    return resurfaced
    94→
    95→
    96→__all__ = ["resurface_stale_skips", "skip_items", "unskip_items"]
    97→

1→"""Queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:
   101→    """Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   102→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   103→
   104→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   105→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   106→
   107→    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   108→    if not issue_ids:
   109→        return 0
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   112→
   113→    # Remove from skipped if present
   114→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    for fid in issue_ids:
   116→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    for fid in issue_ids:
   120→        if fid in order:
   121→            order.remove(fid)
   122→
   123→    # Resolve insertion point
   124→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)
   125→
   126→    # Insert in original order
   127→    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):
   128→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   129→
   130→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   131→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   132→    if position == "top":
   133→        add_promoted_ids(plan, issue_ids)
   134→
   135→    return len(issue_ids)
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = ["move_items"]
   139→
```

    36→def _cmd_triage_complete(
    37→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    38→    *,
    39→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
    42→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    43→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
    44→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    45→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    46→
    47→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    48→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    52→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    53→
    54→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
    55→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    56→
    57→    # Require organize stage confirmed
    58→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
    59→        plan=plan,
    60→        meta=meta,
    61→        stages=stages,
    62→    ):
    63→        return
    64→
    65→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
    66→    if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    67→        plan=plan,
    68→        stages=stages,
    69→        attestation=attestation,
    70→    ):
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
    74→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
    75→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    # Verify cluster coverage
    79→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
    80→
    81→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
    82→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if total > 0 and organized < total:
    87→        remaining = total - organized
    88→        print(
    89→            colorize(
    90→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
    91→                "yellow",
    92→            )
    93→        )
    94→
    95→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
    96→    if strategy is None:
    97→        return
    98→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    # Show summary
   102→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
   103→
   104→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   105→
   106→    # Jump-back guidance before committing
   107→    print()

   113→    )

   123→        },
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)

   128→    print_user_message(

   135→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   136→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   137→    *,
   138→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   139→) -> None:
   140→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   141→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   142→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   143→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   144→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   145→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   146→
   147→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   148→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   152→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   153→
   154→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   155→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    # Determine if this is a light-path (additions only) or full ceremony
   159→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   160→    state = runtime.state
   161→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   162→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   163→
   164→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   165→        stages=stages,
   166→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   167→        si=si,
   168→    ):
   169→        return
   170→
   171→    # Require existing enriched clusters
   172→    clusters_with_issues = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   173→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   174→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   175→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   176→        return
   177→
   178→    # Require note
   179→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    # Require strategy (default to "same" on light path)
   183→    strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   184→        strategy,
   185→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   186→        meta=meta,
   187→    )
   188→    if strategy is None:
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    # Strategy length check (unless "same")
   192→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy):
   193→        return
   194→
   195→    # Require --confirmed with plan review
   196→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   197→        plan=plan,
   198→        state=state,
   199→        confirmed=confirmed,
   200→    )
   201→    if confirmed_text is None:
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    # Validate: note cites at least 1 new/changed issue (if there are any)
   205→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   206→        return
   207→
   208→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   209→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   210→    _record_confirm_existing_completion(
   211→        stages=stages,
   212→        note=note,
   213→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   214→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   215→    )
   216→
   217→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   218→        plan,
   219→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   220→        actor="user",
   221→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   222→    )
   223→
   224→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   225→    print(colorize("  Confirmed existing plan — triage complete.", "green"))
   226→
   227→
   228→def cmd_triage_complete(
   229→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   230→    *,
   231→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   232→) -> None:
   233→    """Public entrypoint for triage completion."""
   234→    _cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   235→

1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    10→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    11→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    12→    purge_ids,
    13→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    16→
    17→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    18→
    19→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    20→
    21→
    22→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    23→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    24→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    25→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    26→
    27→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    28→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    29→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    30→    existing = set(order)
    31→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    32→        if sid not in existing:
    33→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    34→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    35→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    36→        if sid in order:
    37→            order.remove(sid)
    38→    insert_at = 0
    39→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    40→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    41→        insert_at += 1
    42→
    43→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    44→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    45→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    46→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    47→
    48→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    50→    try:
    51→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return []
    54→    cleared: list[str] = []
    55→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    56→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    57→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    59→            cleared.append(later)
    60→    return cleared
    61→
    62→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    63→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    64→    if not cleared:
    65→        return
    66→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    67→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    68→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    69→        None,
    70→    )
    71→    if next_unconfirmed:
    72→        print(colorize(
    73→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    74→            "dim",
    75→        ))
    76→
    77→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    78→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    79→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    80→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    81→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    82→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    83→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    84→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    85→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    86→
    87→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
    89→    return {
    90→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    91→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
    92→    }
    93→
    94→def triage_coverage(
    95→    plan: dict,
    96→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    97→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    98→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
    99→
   100→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   101→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   102→    """
   103→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   104→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for c in clusters.values():
   106→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   107→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   108→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   109→    else:
   110→        review_ids = [
   111→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   112→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   113→        ]
   114→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   115→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   116→
   117→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   118→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   119→    return [
   120→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   121→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   122→    ]
   123→
   124→def apply_completion(
   125→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   126→    plan: dict,
   127→    strategy: str,
   128→    *,
   129→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   130→) -> None:
   131→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   132→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   133→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   134→    state = runtime.state
   135→
   136→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(
   137→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   141→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   142→
   143→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   144→
   145→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   146→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   147→    open_ids = sorted(
   148→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   149→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   150→    )
   151→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_ids
   152→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   153→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   154→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   155→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   156→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   157→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   158→    if stages:
   159→        meta["last_triage"] = {
   160→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   161→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   162→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   163→        }
   164→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   165→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   166→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   167→
   168→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   169→
   170→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("issue_ids")])
   171→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   172→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   173→    if effective_strategy:
   174→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   175→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   176→
   177→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
   178→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
   179→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   180→        if fid in c.get("issue_ids", []):
   181→            return name
   182→    return None
   183→
   184→def count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   185→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
   186→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   187→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   188→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
   189→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
   190→    return dict(counts)
   191→
   192→__all__ = [
   193→    "apply_completion",
   194→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   195→    "count_log_activity_since",
   196→    "find_cluster_for",
   197→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   198→    "inject_triage_stages",
   199→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   200→    "observe_dimension_breakdown",
   201→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   202→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   203→    "purge_triage_stage",
   204→    "triage_coverage",
   205→]
   206→

    84→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    85→    state: dict,
    86→    dim_scores: dict,

    88→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    89→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:

   102→                )
   103→            )
   104→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])

   108→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   160→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   161→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   162→    ]
   163→    return payload
   164→
   165→
   166→def _emit_requested_output(
   167→    opts: NextOptions,
   168→    payload: dict,
   169→    items: list[dict],
   170→) -> bool:
   171→    if opts.output_file:
   172→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   173→            opts.output_file,
   174→            payload,
   175→            len(items),
   176→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   177→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   178→        ):
   179→            return True
   180→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   181→
   182→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(opts.output_format, payload, items):
   183→        return True
   184→    return False
   185→
   186→
   187→def _plan_queue_context(
   188→    *,
   189→    state: dict,
   190→    plan_data: dict | None,
   191→    context=None,
   192→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   193→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   194→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   195→    try:
   196→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   197→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   198→        breakdown = None
   199→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   200→
   201→
   202→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   203→    try:
   204→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   205→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return raw_potentials or None
   207→
   208→
   209→def _build_and_render_queue(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   210→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   211→
   212→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   213→
   214→    # Load the living plan
   215→    plan = load_plan()
   216→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   217→    if (
   218→        plan.get("queue_order")
   219→        or plan.get("overrides")
   220→        or plan.get("clusters")
   221→    ):
   222→        plan_data = plan
   223→
   224→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   225→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   226→    ctx = queue_context(
   227→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   231→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   232→        plan_data,
   233→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   234→        scope=opts.scope,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→    queue = build_work_queue(
   238→        state,
   239→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   240→            count=opts.count,
   241→            scope=opts.scope,
   242→            status=opts.status,
   243→            include_subjective=True,
   244→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   245→            explain=opts.explain,
   246→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   247→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   248→            context=ctx,
   249→        ),
   250→    )
   251→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   252→
   253→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   254→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   255→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   256→        state,
   257→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   258→    )
   259→
   260→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   261→        queue=queue,
   262→        items=items,
   263→        state=state,
   264→        narrative=narrative,
   265→        plan_data=plan_data,
   266→    )
   267→    write_query(payload)
   268→
   269→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   270→        return
   271→
   272→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   273→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   274→        state.get("issues", {}),
   275→        state.get("scan_path"),
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   279→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   280→        state=state,
   281→        plan_data=plan_data,
   282→        context=ctx,
   283→    )
   284→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   285→
   286→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   287→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   288→    if _show_empty_queue(
   289→        queue,
   290→        strict_score,
   291→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   292→        target_strict=target_strict,
   293→    ):
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   297→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   298→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   299→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   300→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   301→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   302→    )
   303→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   304→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   305→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   306→        state,
   307→        dim_scores,
   308→        issues_scoped,
   309→        strict_score=strict_score,
   310→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   311→        queue_total=queue_total,
   312→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   313→        breakdown=breakdown,
   314→    )
   315→    print()
   316→
   317→    if items and plan_data:
   318→        print_user_message(
   319→            "Hey — the task details are printed above. Just start"
   320→            " working on it. When you're done, resolve it with"
   321→            " `desloppify plan resolve` and then run `desloppify"
   322→            " next` again. If this doesn't seem like the right"
   323→            " priority, run `desloppify plan show` to see the full"
   324→            " queue, or `desloppify plan --help` for all plan tools."
   325→            " Don't stop until you're done — unless I've told you"
   326→            " otherwise, just keep going."
   327→        )
   328→
   329→
   330→__all__ = ["NextOptions", "_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   331→

    46→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    48→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    49→    state = runtime.state
    50→
    51→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    52→        return
    53→
    54→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    55→    if config_warning:
    56→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    57→
    58→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    59→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    60→
    61→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    62→    if output:
    63→        try:
    64→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    65→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    66→            print_agent_plan(
    67→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    68→                next_command=next_command,
    69→            )
    70→        except OSError as e:
    71→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    72→    else:
    73→        print(plan_md)
    74→        print()
    75→        print_agent_plan(
    76→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    77→            next_command=next_command,
    78→        )
    79→

    84→

    86→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    87→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    88→    plan = load_plan()
    89→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    90→
    91→    # Dynamic queue count — matches what `next` and `plan queue` show.
    92→    try:
    93→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(runtime.state, plan)
    94→        queue_line = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    95→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    96→        # Fallback to raw plan data if queue build fails
    97→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    98→        queue_line = f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
    99→
   100→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   102→    kind_counts = {
   103→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   104→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   105→    }
   106→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
   107→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
   108→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
   109→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   110→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   111→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   112→
   113→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   114→
   115→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   116→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   117→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   118→    if total_skipped:
   119→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   120→    else:
   121→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   122→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   123→    if clusters:
   124→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   126→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   127→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   130→    if described or noted:
   131→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   132→    if active:
   133→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   134→    if superseded:
   135→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   136→
   137→    # Commit tracking summary
   138→    _cfg = load_config()
   139→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   140→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   141→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   142→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   143→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   144→            print(
   145→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   146→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   147→            )
   148→
   149→
   150→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   151→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   154→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   155→    reset_plan(plan)
   156→    append_log_entry(
   157→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   158→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   159→    )
   160→    save_plan(plan)
   161→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   162→
   163→
   164→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   165→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   166→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   167→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   168→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   169→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   170→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   171→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   172→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   173→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   174→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   175→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   176→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   177→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   178→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   179→}
   180→
   181→
   182→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   183→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   184→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   185→    if plan_action is None:
   186→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   190→    if handler is None:
   191→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   192→        return
   193→    handler(args)
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   196→

    51→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):
    52→    """Raised when review import CLI flags are mutually incompatible."""
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    56→class ReviewImportConfig:
    57→    """Configuration bundle for review import/validate flows."""
    58→
    59→    config: dict | None = None
    60→    allow_partial: bool = False
    61→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False
    62→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None
    63→    attested_external: bool = False
    64→    manual_override: bool = False
    65→    manual_attest: str | None = None
    66→
    67→
    68→def _build_import_load_config(
    69→    *,
    70→    lang_name: str | None,
    71→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
    72→    override_enabled: bool,
    73→    override_attest: str | None,
    74→) -> import_helpers_mod.ImportLoadConfig:
    75→    return import_helpers_mod.ImportLoadConfig(
    76→        lang_name=lang_name,
    77→        allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
    78→        trusted_assessment_source=import_config.trusted_assessment_source,
    79→        trusted_assessment_label=import_config.trusted_assessment_label,
    80→        attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
    81→        manual_override=override_enabled,
    82→        manual_attest=override_attest,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    87→    *,
    88→    attested_external: bool,
    89→    allow_partial: bool,
    90→    override_enabled: bool,
    91→    override_attest: str | None,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    94→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    95→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    96→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    97→        )
    98→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    99→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
   100→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
   101→        )
   102→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   103→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
   104→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
   105→        )
   106→    if override_enabled and (
   107→        not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip()
   108→    ):
   109→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")
   110→
   111→
   112→def _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
   113→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
   114→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   115→    keys: set[str] = set()
   116→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
   117→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
   118→        if normalized:
   119→            keys.add(normalized)
   120→    return keys
   121→
   122→
   123→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   124→    state: dict,
   125→    *,
   126→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   127→) -> int:
   128→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
   129→    if not assessment_keys:
   130→        return 0
   131→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   132→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   133→        return 0
   134→
   135→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   136→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   137→    marked = 0
   138→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   139→        payload = store.get(key)
   140→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   141→            continue
   142→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   143→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   144→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   145→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   146→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   147→        marked += 1
   148→    return marked
   149→
   150→

    18→    show_note_length_requirement,
    19→    validate_attestation,
    20→    validate_note_length,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    23→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    24→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    25→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    26→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import (
    28→    SKIP_KIND_LABELS,
    29→    skip_kind_from_flags,
    30→    skip_kind_requires_attestation,
    31→    skip_kind_requires_note,
    32→    skip_kind_state_status,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    35→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    36→    PLAN_FILE,
    37→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    38→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    39→    annotate_issue,
    40→    append_log_entry,
    41→    clear_focus,
    42→    describe_issue,
    43→    load_plan,
    44→    plan_path_for_state,
    45→    purge_ids,
    46→    purge_uncommitted_ids,
    47→    save_plan,
    48→    set_focus,
    49→    skip_items,
    50→    unskip_items,
    51→)
    52→
    53→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
