```
    55→
    56→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    57→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.STATE_FILE
    58→
    59→
    60→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    61→    return path if path is not None else PLAN_FILE
    62→
    63→
    64→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    65→    if state_file is None:
    66→        return None
    67→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    71→    if not path.exists():
    72→        return None
    73→    return path.read_text()
    74→
    75→
    76→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    77→    if snapshot is None:
    78→        try:
    79→            path.unlink()
    80→        except FileNotFoundError:
    81→            return
    82→        return
    83→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    87→    *,
    88→    plan: dict,
    89→    plan_path: Path | None,
    90→    state_data: dict,
    91→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    94→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    95→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    96→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    97→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    98→
    99→    try:
   100→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
   101→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
   102→    except Exception:
   103→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
   104→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
   105→        raise
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Set augmented description on issues."""
   110→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   111→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   112→        return
   113→
   114→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   115→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
   116→
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   119→    if not issue_ids:
   120→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   121→        return
   122→
   123→    for fid in issue_ids:
   124→        describe_issue(plan, fid, text or None)
   125→    append_log_entry(
   126→        plan, "describe", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   127→        detail={"text": text or None},
   128→    )
   129→    save_plan(plan)
   130→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
   131→
   132→
   133→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   134→    """Set note on issues."""
   135→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   136→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   140→    text: str | None = getattr(args, "text", None)
   141→
   142→    plan = load_plan()
   143→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   144→    if not issue_ids:
   145→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    for fid in issue_ids:
   149→        annotate_issue(plan, fid, text)
   150→    append_log_entry(
   151→        plan, "note", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   152→        note=text,
   153→    )
   154→    save_plan(plan)
   155→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Skip / unskip
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→
   163→def _validate_skip_requirements(
   164→    *,
   165→    kind: str,
   166→    attestation: str | None,
   167→    note: str | None,
   168→) -> bool:
   169→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
   170→        return True
   171→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   172→        show_attestation_requirement(
   173→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   174→            attestation,
   175→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   176→        )
   177→        return False
   178→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
   179→        print(
   180→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→        return False
   184→    return True
   185→
   186→
   187→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   188→    *,
   189→    kind: str,
   190→    state_file: Path | None,
   191→    issue_ids: list[str],
   192→    note: str | None,
   193→    attestation: str | None,
   194→) -> dict | None:
   195→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   196→    if status is None:
   197→        return None
   198→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   199→    for fid in issue_ids:
   200→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
   201→            state_data,
   202→            fid,
   203→            status,
   204→            note or "",
   205→            attestation=attestation,
   206→        )
   207→    return state_data
   208→
   209→
   210→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   212→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   219→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   220→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   221→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   222→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   223→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   224→
   225→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   226→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   227→        kind=kind,
   228→        attestation=attestation,
   229→        note=note,
   230→    ):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   234→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   235→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   236→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   237→    if not issue_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   242→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   243→        kind=kind,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   246→        note=note,
   247→        attestation=attestation,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   251→    count = skip_items(
   252→        plan,
   253→        issue_ids,
   254→        kind=kind,
   255→        reason=reason,
   256→        note=note,
   257→        attestation=attestation,
   258→        review_after=review_after,
   259→        scan_count=scan_count,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Log the skip action
   263→    append_log_entry(
   264→        plan,
   265→        "skip",
   266→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   267→        actor="user",
   268→        note=note,
   269→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   270→    )
   271→    if state_data is not None:
   272→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   273→            plan=plan,
   274→            plan_path=plan_file,
   275→            state_data=state_data,
   276→            state_path_value=state_file,
   277→        )
   278→    else:
   279→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   280→
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   282→    if review_after:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   284→    print_user_message(
   285→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→
   292→
   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   301→
   302→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   303→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   304→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   305→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   306→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   307→    if not issue_ids:
   308→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   309→        return
   310→
   311→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   312→    append_log_entry(
   313→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   314→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   315→    )
   316→
   317→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   318→    reopened: list[str] = []
   319→    if need_reopen:
   320→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   321→        for fid in need_reopen:
   322→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   323→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   324→            plan=plan,
   325→            plan_path=plan_file,
   326→            state_data=state_data,
   327→            state_path_value=state_file,
   328→        )
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   332→
   333→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   334→
   335→
   336→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   337→# Reopen
   338→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339→
   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:
   388→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   389→
   390→
   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
   484→
   485→
   486→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   487→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   488→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   489→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   490→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   491→
   492→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   493→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   494→        if not note:
   495→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   496→            return
   497→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   498→        args.attest = attestation
   499→
   500→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   501→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   502→    if synthetic_ids:
   503→        plan = load_plan()
   504→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   505→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   506→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   507→            if sid in blocked_map:
   508→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   509→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   510→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   511→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   512→                    return
   513→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   514→        append_log_entry(
   515→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   516→        )
   517→        save_plan(plan)
   518→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   519→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   520→        if not real_patterns:
   521→            return
   522→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   523→        patterns = real_patterns
   524→        args.patterns = patterns
   525→
   526→    # Validate note length
   527→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   528→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   532→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   533→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   534→        return
   535→
   536→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   537→    try:
   538→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   539→        state = runtime.state
   540→        plan = load_plan()
   541→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   542→            return
   543→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   544→        plan = None
   545→
   546→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   547→    try:
   548→        if plan is None:
   549→            plan = load_plan()
   550→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   551→        cluster_name = None
   552→        for p in patterns:
   553→            if p in clusters:
   554→                cluster_name = p
   555→                break
   556→        append_log_entry(
   557→            plan,
   558→            "done",
   559→            issue_ids=patterns,
   560→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   561→            actor="user",
   562→            note=note,
   563→        )
   564→        save_plan(plan)
   565→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   566→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   567→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   568→
   569→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   570→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   571→        status="fixed",
   572→        patterns=patterns,
   573→        note=note,
   574→        attest=attestation,
   575→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   576→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   577→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   578→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   579→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   580→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   581→    )
   582→
   583→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   584→
   585→
   586→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   587→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   588→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   589→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   590→
   591→    plan = load_plan()
   592→    if clear_flag:
   593→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   594→        clear_focus(plan)
   595→        append_log_entry(
   596→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   597→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   598→        )
   599→        save_plan(plan)
   600→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   601→        return
   602→
   603→    if not cluster_name:
   604→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   605→        if active:
   606→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   607→        else:
   608→            print("  No active focus.")
   609→        return
   610→
   611→    try:
   612→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   613→    except ValueError as ex:
   614→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   615→        return
   616→    append_log_entry(
   617→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   618→        detail={"action": "set"},
   619→    )
   620→    save_plan(plan)
   621→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   622→
   623→
   624→__all__ = [
   625→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   626→    "cmd_plan_resolve",
   627→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   628→    "cmd_plan_note",
   629→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   630→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   631→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   632→]
   633→
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import (
    10→    write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    14→
    15→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    21→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its issue IDs."""
    22→    try:
    23→        if not has_living_plan():
    24→            return None
    25→        plan = load_plan()
    26→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    27→        if cluster and cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    28→            return list(cluster["issue_ids"])
    29→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    30→        _logger.debug("cluster expansion skipped for %r", pattern, exc_info=True)
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    35→    state: dict,
    36→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    37→    *,
    38→    attestation: str | None,
    39→) -> list[str]:
    40→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    41→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    42→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    43→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    44→        if cluster_ids:
    45→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    46→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    47→                    state,
    48→                    fid,
    49→                    args.status,
    50→                    args.note,
    51→                    attestation=attestation,
    52→                )
    53→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    54→            continue
    55→
    56→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    57→            state,
    58→            pattern,
    59→            args.status,
    60→            args.note,
    61→            attestation=attestation,
    62→        )
    63→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    64→    return all_resolved
    65→
    66→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    67→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    68→    _write_query_best_effort(
    69→        {
    70→            "command": "resolve",
    71→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    72→            "status": context.status,
    73→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    74→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    75→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    76→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    77→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    78→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    79→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    80→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    81→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    82→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    83→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    84→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    85→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    86→        },
    87→        context="resolve query payload update",
    88→    )
    89→
```

```
1→"""Resolve command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→from typing import NamedTuple
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import require_triage_current_or_exit
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    18→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    19→    add_uncommitted_issues,
    20→    append_log_entry,
    21→    has_living_plan,
    22→    load_plan,
    23→    purge_ids,
    24→    purge_uncommitted_ids,
    25→    save_plan,
    26→)
    27→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    28→from desloppify.state import coerce_assessment_score
    29→
    30→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.persist import save_state_or_exit
    31→
    32→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    33→from .queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
    34→from .render import (
    35→    _print_next_command,
    36→    _print_resolve_summary,
    37→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    38→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    39→    render_commit_guidance,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.attestation import (
    42→    show_note_length_requirement,
    43→    validate_note_length,
    44→)
    45→
    46→from .selection import (
    47→    ResolveQueryContext,
    48→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,
    49→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    50→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    51→)
    52→
    53→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→
    56→class ClusterContext(NamedTuple):
    57→    cluster_name: str | None
    58→    cluster_completed: bool
    59→    cluster_remaining: int
    60→
    61→
    62→def _capture_cluster_context(
    63→    plan: dict, resolved_ids: list[str],
    64→) -> ClusterContext:
    65→    """Determine cluster membership for resolved issues, pre-purge."""
    66→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") or {}
    67→    overrides = plan.get("overrides") or {}
    68→    # Find the cluster the first resolved issue belongs to
    69→    cluster_name: str | None = None
    70→    for rid in resolved_ids:
    71→        ov = overrides.get(rid)
    72→        if ov and ov.get("cluster"):
    73→            cluster_name = ov["cluster"]
    74→            break
    75→    if not cluster_name or cluster_name not in clusters:
    76→        return ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    77→    # Count how many will remain after these ids are removed
    78→    current_ids = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids") or [])
    79→    remaining = current_ids - set(resolved_ids)
    80→    return ClusterContext(
    81→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
    82→        cluster_completed=len(remaining) == 0,
    83→        cluster_remaining=len(remaining),
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    88→    if args.status != "fixed":
    89→        return True
    90→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    91→    if validate_note_length(note):
    92→        return True
    93→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    94→    return False
    95→
    96→
    97→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    98→    *,
    99→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   100→    all_resolved: list[str],
   101→    attestation: str | None,
   102→) -> tuple[dict | None, ClusterContext]:
   103→    plan = None
   104→    ctx = ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
   105→    try:
   106→        if not has_living_plan():
   107→            return None, ctx
   108→        plan = load_plan()
   109→        ctx = _capture_cluster_context(plan, all_resolved)
   110→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   111→        append_log_entry(
   112→            plan,
   113→            "resolve",
   114→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
   115→            actor="user",
   116→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
   117→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
   118→        )
   119→        if ctx.cluster_completed and ctx.cluster_name:
   120→            append_log_entry(
   121→                plan,
   122→                "cluster_done",
   123→                issue_ids=all_resolved,
   124→                cluster_name=ctx.cluster_name,
   125→                actor="user",
   126→            )
   127→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
   128→        if args.status == "fixed":
   129→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
   130→        elif args.status == "open":
   131→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   132→        save_plan(plan)
   133→        if purged:
   134→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   135→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   136→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
   137→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   138→    return plan, ctx
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   143→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   144→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   145→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    state_file = state_path(args)
   149→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   150→
```

```
140→
   141→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   143→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   144→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   145→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    state_file = state_path(args)
   149→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   150→
   151→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    if args.status == "fixed":
   155→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   156→            state=state,
   157→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   158→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   162→        state,
   163→        args,
   164→        attestation=attestation,
   165→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   166→    )
   167→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   168→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   169→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   170→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   171→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   175→    if not all_resolved:
   176→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   177→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   181→
   182→    plan, cluster_ctx = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   183→        args=args,
   184→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   185→        attestation=attestation,
   186→    )
   187→    mid_cluster = (
   188→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   189→    )
   190→
   191→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   192→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   193→        state,
   194→        status=args.status,
   195→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   196→    )
   197→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   198→    if not mid_cluster:
   199→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   200→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   201→        args=args,
   202→        state=state,
   203→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   204→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   208→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   209→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   210→        state,
   211→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   212→    )
   213→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   214→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   215→
   216→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   217→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   218→        ResolveQueryContext(
   219→            patterns=args.patterns,
   220→            status=args.status,
   221→            resolved=all_resolved,
   222→            next_command=next_command,
   223→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   224→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   225→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   226→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   227→            attestation=attestation,
   228→            narrative=narrative,
   229→            state=state,
   230→        )
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    if args.status == "fixed" and plan is not None and next_command == "desloppify next":
   234→        if mid_cluster:
   235→            print_user_message(
   236→                f"Hey — task done, {cluster_ctx.cluster_remaining} left in"
   237→                f" cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'. Run `desloppify next`"
   238→                " to pick up the next one. Keep going — don't stop until the"
   239→                " cluster is finished."
```

```
     7→
     8→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
     9→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
    10→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
    11→
    12→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    13→        return None
    14→
    15→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
    16→
    17→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    18→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
    19→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
    20→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
    21→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""

   106→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   107→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   108→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   109→
   110→    return {
   111→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   112→        "tier": 1,
   113→        "confidence": "high",
   114→        "detector": "workflow",
   115→        "file": ".",
   116→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   117→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   118→        "detail": {
   119→            "strict": strict,
   120→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   121→            "delta": delta,
   122→        },
   123→        "primary_command": (
   124→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   125→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   126→        ),
   127→        "blocked_by": [],
   128→        "is_blocked": False,
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→__all__ = [
   133→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   134→    "build_create_plan_item",
   135→    "build_import_scores_item",
   136→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   137→]
   138→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    16→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    17→
    18→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    19→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    20→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    21→})
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    26→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    27→
    28→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    29→    objective_count: int  # how many
    30→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    31→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    32→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    33→
    34→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    35→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    36→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    37→            return True  # unconditional
    38→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    39→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    40→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    41→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    42→        return False
    43→
    44→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    45→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    46→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    47→            return False  # never evict unscored
    48→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    49→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    50→        return False
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    54→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    55→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    60→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    61→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    62→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    63→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    64→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    65→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    66→            return True
    67→    return False
    68→
    69→
    70→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = object()
    71→
    72→
    73→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    74→    state: StateModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    77→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    78→    plan: dict | None = None,
    79→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    80→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    81→
    82→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    83→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    84→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    85→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    86→
    87→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    88→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    89→    """
    90→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import (
    91→        current_stale_ids,
    92→        current_under_target_ids,
    93→        current_unscored_ids,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
    97→        state.get("scan_path")
    98→        if scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY
    99→        else scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   103→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   104→
   105→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   106→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   107→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   108→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   109→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   110→    objective_count = sum(
   111→        1
   112→        for fid, f in issues.items()
   113→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   114→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   115→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   116→        and not _is_evidence_only(f)
   117→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(f.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   118→        and fid not in skipped_ids
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   122→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   123→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   124→
   125→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   126→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   127→        objective_count=objective_count,
   128→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   129→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   130→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   136→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   137→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   138→]
   139→
```

```
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.

    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE

    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:

    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty

    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,

    93→    )
```

```
   130→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   131→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   132→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   133→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   134→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   135→    if not has_triage:
   136→        return ""
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   139→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   140→    if completed:
   141→        return (
   142→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   143→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   144→        )
   145→    return (
   146→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   147→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   148→    )
   149→
```

```
1→"""Integration tests for lifecycle transitions through reconcile → work queue.
     2→
     3→Exercises the full lifecycle: scan → initial reviews → score → plan → triage → objectives.
     4→Each test calls ``reconcile_plan_post_scan`` directly, then ``build_work_queue`` to verify
     5→the queue shows the right items in the right order at each lifecycle stage.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from types import SimpleNamespace
    12→
    13→import pytest
```

# Triage completion

plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_completed_at"] = state_mod.utc_now()

# Synthetic item completion
plan_mod.purge_ids(plan, ["workflow::communicate-score"])

# Skip and resurface
plan_mod.skip_items(plan, ["issue-id"], kind="temporary", review_after=3, scan_count=state["scan_count"])
count, reopen_ids = plan_mod.unskip_items(plan, ["issue-id"])

# Save both

plan_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_file)

EXACT FUNCTION SIGNATURES & IMPORT PATHS
=========================================

## Primary Completion Functions

### 1. cmd_plan_resolve - Main Command Handler
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:486
Signature:
  def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None

Called from: CLI dispatcher (desloppify plan resolve <patterns>)
Takes args attributes:
  - patterns: list[str]
  - note: str | None
  - attest: str | None (attestation)
  - confirm: bool (auto-generates attestation from note)
  - force_resolve: bool (bypass triage guardrail)

Internal flow:
  1. Separates synthetic IDs from real patterns
  2. For synthetic (triage::, workflow::, subjective::):
     - Validates triage dependencies via _blocked_triage_stages()
     - Calls purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
     - Logs with append_log_entry(plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, ...)
  3. For real patterns:
     - Validates note length via validate_note_length()
     - Validates attestation via validate_attestation()
     - Builds resolve_args Namespace with status="fixed"
     - Delegates to cmd_resolve(resolve_args)

### 2. cmd_resolve - Full Issue Resolution
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py:141
Signature:
  def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None

Takes args attributes:
  - patterns: list[str]
  - status: str ("fixed", "wontfix", "open")
  - note: str | None
  - attest: str | None
  - force_resolve: bool
  - state: str | None (file path override)

Internal flow:
  1. Validates resolve inputs via _validate_resolve_inputs()
  2. Checks queue guards via _check_queue_order_guard()
  3. For status=="fixed": enforces triage current via require_triage_current_or_exit()
  4. Captures previous scores for comparison
  5. Calls _resolve_all_patterns() → state_mod.resolve_issues() for each pattern
  6. Saves state via save_state_or_exit()
  7. Updates living plan via _update_living_plan_after_resolve():
     - Captures cluster context
     - Calls purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
     - Logs "resolve" entry + "cluster_done" if cluster completes
     - For status=="fixed": calls add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
     - For status=="open": calls purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
  8. Renders summary, scores, cluster guidance

### 3. resolve_issues - State Layer Mutation
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py:99
Signature:
  def resolve_issues(
      state: StateModel,
      pattern: str,
      status: str,  # "fixed", "wontfix", "open"
      note: str | None = None,
      attestation: str | None = None,
  ) -> list[str]

Returns: list of resolved issue IDs

Mutations per issue:
  - issue["status"] = status
  - issue["resolved_at"] = utc_now()
  - issue["suppressed"] = False
  - issue["suppression_pattern"] = None
  - issue["suppressed_at"] = None
  - issue["note"] = note
  - issue["resolution_attestation"] = {
      "kind": "manual",
      "text": attestation,
      "attested_at": utc_now(),
      "scan_verified": False
    }
  
  If status == "wontfix":
    - issue["wontfix_scan_count"] = state.get("scan_count", 0)
    - issue["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
        "captured_at": utc_now(),
        "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
        "tier": issue.get("tier"),
        "confidence": issue.get("confidence"),
        "detail": copy.deepcopy(issue.get("detail", {}))
      }
  
  If status == "open":
    - issue["reopen_count"] = int(issue.get("reopen_count", 0)) + 1
    - issue.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
    - issue.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
    - issue["resolution_attestation"]["kind"] = "manual_reopen"
    - issue["resolution_attestation"]["previous_status"] = previous_status

Also calls: _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve() if detector=="review"
  - Sets needs_review_refresh=True on matching subjective_assessments
  - Sets stale_since timestamp

### 4. purge_ids - Plan Queue Removal
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_lifecycle.py:43
Signature:
  def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int

Returns: count of IDs actually present

Mutations:
  - Removes from plan["queue_order"]
  - Removes from plan["skipped"] dict keys
  - Removes from all plan["clusters"][*]["issue_ids"]
  - Prunes promoted IDs via prune_promoted_ids()
  - For overrides: sets plan["overrides"][fid]["cluster"] = None

### 5. append_log_entry - Execution Log
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_meta.py
Signature:
  def append_log_entry(
      plan: PlanModel,
      action: str,
      *,
      issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
      cluster_name: str | None = None,
      actor: str = "auto",
      note: str | None = None,
      detail: dict | None = None,
  ) -> None

Appends to plan["execution_log"]:
  {
    "timestamp": utc_now(),
    "action": action,
    "issue_ids": issue_ids or [],
    "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    "actor": actor,
    "note": note,
    "detail": detail or {}
  }


## Skip/Unskip Operations

File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py:12
Signature:

      kind: str = "temporary",  # "permanent", "false_positive"
      reason: str | None = None,
      note: str | None = None,
      attestation: str | None = None,
      review_after: int | None = None,
      scan_count: int = 0,
  ) -> int

Returns: count skipped

Creates plan["skipped"][fid]:
  {
    "issue_id": fid,
    "kind": kind,
    "reason": reason,
    "note": note,
    "attestation": attestation,
    "created_at": utc_now(),
    "review_after": review_after,
    "skipped_at_scan": scan_count
  }

### 7. unskip_items - Restore from Skipped
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py:46
Signature:
  def unskip_items(
      plan: PlanModel,
      issue_ids: list[str]
  ) -> tuple[int, list[str]]

Returns: (count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)

Restores to plan["queue_order"] and removes from plan["skipped"]

## Triage Completion

### 8. cmd_triage_complete - Full Triage Workflow
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_completion_commands.py:36
Signature:
  def cmd_triage_complete(
      args: argparse.Namespace,
      *,
      services: TriageServices | None = None,
  ) -> None

Validates:
  - has_triage_in_queue(plan)
  - _require_organize_stage_for_complete()
  - _completion_clusters_valid(plan)
  - Cluster coverage > 0
  
Then calls: apply_completion()

### 9. apply_completion - Shared Triage Completion Logic
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/helpers.py:124
Signature:
  def apply_completion(
      args: argparse.Namespace,
      plan: dict,
      strategy: str,
      *,
      services: TriageServices | None = None,
  ) -> None

Mutations:
  1. Calls purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
  2. Sets plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
  3. Sets plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triaged_ids"] = [open review IDs]
  4. If strategy != "same": sets plan["epic_triage_meta"]["strategy_summary"] = strategy
  5. Archives stages to plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_triage"]
  6. Clears plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"] = {}
  7. Calls resolved_services.save_plan(plan)

### 10. cmd_confirm_existing - Fast-track Triage
File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_completion_commands.py:135
Signature:
  def cmd_confirm_existing(
      args: argparse.Namespace,
      *,
      services: TriageServices | None = None,
  ) -> None

Validates:
  - has_triage_in_queue(plan)
  - _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta)
  - _confirm_existing_stages_valid()
  - _confirm_note_valid(note)
  - _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy)
  
Then:
  - Calls _record_confirm_existing_completion()
  - Calls apply_completion()

## Constants & IDs

Synthetic IDs (do NOT get resolved in state, only purged from plan):
- triage::observe
- triage::reflect
- triage::organize
- triage::commit
- workflow::score-checkpoint
- workflow::create-plan
- workflow::import-scores
- workflow::communicate-score
- subjective::<dimension-slug>  (e.g. subjective::naming_quality)

Real Issue Patterns (GET resolved in state):
- "pattern" (glob)
- "issue-id" (exact)
- "detector::*" (e.g. "review::*")
- Cluster names (expand to member IDs)

Skip Kinds:
- "temporary" (resurfaces after review_after scans)
- "permanent" (requires --attest, needs state reopen to uncheck)
- "false_positive" (requires --attest, needs state reopen to uncheck)

## Example 1: Complete a Regular Issue

```python
from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
from pathlib import Path

# Load existing state and plan
state = state_mod.load_state(Path(".desloppify/state-python.json"))
plan = plan_mod.load_plan()

# Mark issue as fixed in state layer
resolved_ids = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    state,
    pattern="complexity-high-src-app-py",
    status="fixed",
    note="Refactored logic for clarity",
    attestation="I have refactored logic for clarity and I am not gaming the score."
)
# Returns: ["complexity-high-src-app-py"]

# Remove from plan queue (after state is updated)
count = plan_mod.purge_ids(plan, resolved_ids)
# Returns: 1 (was in 1 location)

# Log the completion
plan_mod.append_log_entry(
    plan,
    "resolve",
    issue_ids=resolved_ids,
    actor="user",
    note="Refactored logic for clarity",
    detail={"status": "fixed", "attestation": "..."}
)

# Save both
state_mod.save_state(state, Path(".desloppify/state-python.json"))
plan_mod.save_plan(plan)

print(f"Resolved {len(resolved_ids)} issue(s)")
```

## Example 8: Collect State Mutations for Assertion

```python
from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
from pathlib import Path

# Load fresh copies
state = state_mod.load_state()
plan = plan_mod.load_plan()

# Record state before
before_queue_count = len(plan["queue_order"])
before_skipped_count = len(plan["skipped"])
before_issue_status = state["issues"].get("complexity-high", {}).get("status")

# Perform completion
resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(state, "complexity-high", "fixed", "Done", "I am certain.")
plan_mod.purge_ids(plan, resolved)
plan_mod.append_log_entry(plan, "resolve", issue_ids=resolved, actor="user")

# Check results
assert resolved == ["complexity-high"], f"Expected 1 resolved, got {resolved}"
assert len(plan["queue_order"]) == before_queue_count - 1
assert state["issues"]["complexity-high"]["status"] == "fixed"
assert state["issues"]["complexity-high"]["resolved_at"] is not None
assert state["issues"]["complexity-high"]["resolution_attestation"]["text"] == "I am certain."

print("All assertions passed!")
```

## Example 9: Full Lifecycle Test

```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import build_work_queue, QueueBuildOptions
import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
from pathlib import Path

def test_complete_lifecycle():
    # 1. Create state with issues
    state = {
        "scan_count": 1,
        "scan_path": "src",
        "issues": {
            "complexity-high": {
                "id": "complexity-high",
                "detector": "complexity",
                "status": "open",
                "file": "src/app.py",
                "tier": 2,
                "confidence": "high",
                "summary": "High cyclomatic complexity"
            },
            "review-test": {
                "id": "review-test",
                "detector": "review",
                "status": "open",
                "file": "src/app.py",
                "detail": {"dimension": "naming_quality"},
                "summary": "Variable naming unclear"
            }
        },
        "dimension_scores": {},
        "subjective_assessments": {},
    }
    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
    state_mod.validate_state_invariants(state)
    
    # 2. Create and reconcile plan
    plan = empty_plan()
    result = reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(state, plan)
    
    # 3. Check queue contains both issues
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(count=10, include_subjective=True)
    )
    items = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
    assert "complexity-high" in items
    
    # 4. Resolve complexity issue
    resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
        state, "complexity-high", "fixed",
        note="Fixed", attestation="Done."
    )
    plan_mod.purge_ids(plan, resolved)
    plan_mod.append_log_entry(plan, "resolve", issue_ids=resolved, actor="user")
    
    # 5. Verify queue shrunk
    queue = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(count=10))
    items = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
    assert "complexity-high" not in items
    
    print("Lifecycle test passed!")

test_complete_lifecycle()
```

Perfect! Now let me create a final summary to share with the user:

## Summary

I've completed a comprehensive search of the desloppify CLI codebase to understand how work queue items get marked as completed/resolved. Here are the key findings:

## **Key Entry Points for Marking Items Complete**

### 1. **Main Command Handler: `cmd_plan_resolve`**
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` (lines 486-583)
- **Signature**: `cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None`
- **Handles**:
  - Synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) → directly purges from plan
  - Real issues → delegates to `cmd_resolve` for full state + plan resolution

### 2. **State Layer Resolution: `resolve_issues`**
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` (line 99)
- **Signature**: 
```python
def resolve_issues(
    state: StateModel,
    pattern: str,
    status: str,  # "fixed", "wontfix", "open"
    note: str | None = None,
    attestation: str | None = None,
) -> list[str]
```
- **Mutations**:
  - Sets `issue["status"]`, `issue["resolved_at"]`, `issue["resolution_attestation"]`
  - For wontfix: captures `wontfix_snapshot` with current state
  - For review issues: marks subjective assessments as stale
  - Returns: list of affected issue IDs

### 3. **Plan Queue Removal: `purge_ids`**
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_lifecycle.py` (line 43)
- **Signature**: `def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int`
- **Removes from**:
  - `plan["queue_order"]`
  - `plan["skipped"]` dict
  - All `plan["clusters"][*]["issue_ids"]`

## **Specific Item Type Handling**

| Item Type | ID Format | Completion Path | State Changes |
|-----------|-----------|---|---|
| **Triage Stages** | `triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::commit` | `purge_ids(plan, [sid])` | None (plan-only) |
| **Workflow Items** | `workflow::score-checkpoint`, `workflow::create-plan`, `workflow::import-scores`, `workflow::communicate-score` | `purge_ids(plan, [workflow_id])` | None (plan-only) |
| **Subjective Dimensions** | `subjective::<dimension-slug>` (e.g., `subjective::naming_quality`) | `purge_ids(plan, [subjective_id])` | Assessment updated/stale marked |
| **Regular Issues** | `review-123`, `complexity::high`, etc. | `resolve_issues()` + `purge_ids()` | Status → fixed/wontfix/open, timestamps added |

## **Triage Stage Completion**

- **Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_completion_commands.py`
- **Main Function**: `apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=None)`
- **Mutations**:
  1. `purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))` - removes all 4 stages
  2. Sets `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_completed_at"]`
  3. Archives previous stages to `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_triage"]`
  4. Clears `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]`

## **Skip Operations**

- **Skip**: `skip_items(plan, issue_ids, kind="temporary|permanent|false_positive", review_after=N, ...)`
  - Creates entry in `plan["skipped"]` with metadata
  - Temporary skips resurface after `review_after` scans
  
- **Unskip**: `unskip_items(plan, issue_ids) -> tuple[int, list[str]]`
  - Returns count unskipped + IDs needing state reopen (permanent/false_positive)

## **Key Mutation Functions for Tests**

```python
# Import all these for test helpers
from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import resolve_issues
from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_lifecycle import purge_ids
from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import skip_items, unskip_items
from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS, WORKFLOW_*

# Mark issue complete
state_mod.resolve_issues(state, "pattern", "fixed", "note", attestation="...")
plan_mod.purge_ids(plan, ["issue-id"])

# Log completion
plan_mod.append_log_entry(plan, "done", issue_ids=["..."], actor="user", note="...")

# Skip/resurface
plan_mod.skip_items(plan, ["id"], kind="temporary", review_after=3, scan_count=state["scan_count"])
count, reopen_ids = plan_mod.unskip_items(plan, ["id"])
```

```
     2→
```

```
     6→import sys

     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime

    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    28→class _DetectorNamesCacheCompat:
    29→    """Compat shim for tests that poke the legacy detector-name cache."""
    30→
    31→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    32→        self._store: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    33→
    34→    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:
    35→        return key in self._store
    36→
    37→    def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> list[str]:
    38→        return self._store[key]
    39→
    40→    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: list[str]) -> None:
    41→        self._store[key] = value
    42→
    43→    def pop(self, key: str, default=None):
    44→        return self._store.pop(key, default)
    45→
    46→
    47→_DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE = _DetectorNamesCacheCompat()
    48→
    49→
    50→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    51→def _get_detector_names_cached() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    52→    """Compute detector names once until cache invalidation."""
    53→    return tuple(detector_names())
    54→
    55→
    56→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    57→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    58→    return list(_get_detector_names_cached())
    59→
    60→
    61→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    62→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    63→    _get_detector_names_cached.cache_clear()
    64→    _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE.pop("names", None)
    65→
    66→
    67→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    68→
    69→
    70→def create_parser():
    71→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    72→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    73→
    74→
    75→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    76→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    77→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    78→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    79→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    80→    if not combined:
    81→        return
    82→    set_exclusions(combined)
    83→    if cli_exclusions:
    84→        print(
    85→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    86→            file=sys.stderr,
    87→        )
    88→        return
    89→    print(
    90→        colorize(
    91→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    92→        ),
    93→        file=sys.stderr,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→
    97→def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    98→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    99→
   100→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
   101→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
   102→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
   103→    """
   104→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
   105→        return
   106→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
   107→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
   108→        try:
   109→            state_file = state_path(args)
   110→            if state_file:
   111→                saved = load_state(state_file)
   112→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
   113→                if saved_path:
   114→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
   115→                    return
   116→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   117→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
   118→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   119→    if lang:
   120→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   121→    else:
   122→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   123→
   124→
   125→def _load_shared_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   126→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   127→    config = load_config()
   128→
   129→    state_file = state_path(args)
   130→    state = load_state(state_file)
   131→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   132→
   133→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   137→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   138→
   139→
   140→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   141→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   142→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   143→    try:
   144→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   145→    except SystemExit:
   146→        return
   147→
   148→
   149→def main() -> None:
   150→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   151→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   152→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   153→            try:
   154→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   155→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   156→                logger.debug(
   157→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   158→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   159→                )
   160→
   161→    parser = create_parser()
   162→    args = parser.parse_args()
   163→    if args.command == "help":
   164→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    try:
   168→        with runtime_scope():
   169→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   170→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   171→
   172→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   173→            handler(args)
   174→    except CommandError as exc:
   175→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   176→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   177→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   178→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   179→        sys.exit(1)
   180→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   181→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   182→        sys.exit(1)
   183→
   184→
   185→if __name__ == "__main__":
   186→    main()
   187→
```

```
1→"""Path resolution and exclusion matching helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import os
     7→import tempfile
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    11→
    12→
    13→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
    14→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern."""
    15→    parts = Path(rel_path).parts
    16→    if exclusion in parts:
    17→        return True
    18→    if "*" in exclusion:
    19→        if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(part, exclusion) for part in parts):
    20→            return True
    21→        # Full-path glob match for patterns with directory separators
    22→        # (e.g. "Wan2GP/**" should match "Wan2GP/models/rf.py").
    23→        if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    24→            normalized_path = rel_path.lstrip("./")
    25→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(normalized_path, exclusion):
    26→                return True
    27→    if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    28→        normalized = exclusion.rstrip("/").rstrip(os.sep)
    29→        return rel_path == normalized or rel_path.startswith(normalized + "/") or rel_path.startswith(
    30→            normalized + os.sep
    31→        )
    32→    return False
    33→
    34→
    35→def normalize_path_separators(path: str) -> str:
    36→    return path.replace("\\", "/")
    37→
    38→
    39→def safe_relpath(path: str | Path, start: str | Path) -> str:
    40→    try:
    41→        return os.path.relpath(str(path), str(start))
    42→    except ValueError:
    43→        return str(Path(path).resolve())
    44→
    45→
    46→def rel(path: str) -> str:
    47→    root = get_project_root()
    48→    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    49→    try:
    50→        return normalize_path_separators(str(resolved.relative_to(root)))
    51→    except ValueError:
    52→        return normalize_path_separators(safe_relpath(resolved, root))
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_path(filepath: str) -> str:
    56→    """Resolve a filepath to absolute, handling both relative and absolute."""
    57→    p = Path(filepath)
    58→    if p.is_absolute():
    59→        return str(p.resolve())
    60→    return str((get_project_root() / filepath).resolve())
    61→
    62→
    63→def resolve_scan_file(
    64→    filepath: str | Path,
    65→    *,
    66→    scan_root: str | Path | None = None,
    67→) -> Path:
    68→    """Resolve a scan file path with explicit scan-root-first semantics.
    69→
    70→    Relative file paths are resolved against ``scan_root`` first (when provided)
    71→    and then against the process project root as a fallback.
    72→    """
    73→    p = Path(filepath)
    74→    if p.is_absolute():
    75→        return p.resolve()
    76→
    77→    root = get_project_root()
    78→    if scan_root is not None:
    79→        scan_root_path = Path(scan_root)
    80→        scan_root_abs = (
    81→            scan_root_path.resolve()
    82→            if scan_root_path.is_absolute()
    83→            else (root / scan_root_path).resolve()
    84→        )
    85→        scan_candidate = (scan_root_abs / p).resolve()
    86→        if scan_candidate.exists():
    87→            return scan_candidate
    88→
    89→    return (root / p).resolve()
    90→
    91→
    92→def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    93→    """Atomically write text to a file using temp+rename."""
    94→    p = Path(filepath)
    95→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    96→    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    97→    try:
    98→        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
    99→            f.write(content)
   100→        os.replace(tmp, str(p))
   101→    except OSError:
   102→        if os.path.exists(tmp):
   103→            os.unlink(tmp)
   104→        raise
   105→
   106→
   107→def count_lines(path: Path) -> int:
   108→    """Count lines in a file without loading full contents into memory."""
   109→    count = 0
   110→    try:
   111→        with path.open("rb") as handle:
   112→            for _ in handle:
   113→                count += 1
   114→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   115→        return 0
   116→    return count
   117→
   118→
   119→__all__ = [
   120→    "count_lines",
   121→    "matches_exclusion",
   122→    "normalize_path_separators",
   123→    "rel",
   124→    "resolve_path",
   125→    "resolve_scan_file",
   126→    "safe_relpath",
   127→    "safe_write_text",
   128→]
   129→
```

```
     2→
     3→Plan internals live in ``desloppify.engine._plan``; this module exposes
     4→the stable, non-private API used by commands and rendering helpers.
     5→"""
     6→

     8→
     9→# --- auto-clustering --------------------------------------------------------
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import (
    11→    AUTO_PREFIX,
    12→    auto_cluster_issues,
    13→)

   129→
   130→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   131→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   132→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   133→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   134→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   135→    if not has_triage:
   136→        return ""
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   139→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   140→    if completed:
   141→        return (
   142→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   143→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   144→        )
   145→    return (
   146→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   147→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   148→    )
   149→
```
