1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→import argparse
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→
    11→
    12→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    13→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    14→    state_dir = get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
    15→    if not state_dir.exists():
    16→        return None
    17→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    18→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    19→        return candidates[0]
    20→    return None
    21→
    22→
    23→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    24→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    25→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    26→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    27→
    28→
    29→def state_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path | None:
    30→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    31→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    32→    if path_arg:
    33→        return Path(path_arg)
    34→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    35→    if not lang_name:
    36→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    37→    if lang_name:
    38→        resolved = get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    39→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    40→            return resolved
    41→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    42→        if fallback is not None:
    43→            return fallback
    44→        return resolved
    45→
    46→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    47→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    48→        if fallback is not None:
    49→            return fallback
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    55→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    56→    if not has_completed_scan:
    57→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    58→    return has_completed_scan
    59→

   336→        "score_confidence": {},
   337→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   338→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   339→    }
   340→
   341→
   342→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   343→    try:
   344→        parsed = int(value)
   345→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   346→        return default
   347→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   348→
   349→
   350→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
   351→    if old not in d:
   352→        return False
   353→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
   354→    return True
   355→
   356→
   357→def migrate_state_keys(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   358→    """Migrate legacy key names in-place.
   359→
   360→    - ``"findings"`` → ``"issues"``
   361→    - ``dimension_scores[dim]["issues"]`` → ``"failing"``
   362→    """
   363→    state_dict = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   364→    _rename_key(state_dict, "findings", "issues")
   365→
   366→    for ds in state_dict.get("dimension_scores", {}).values():
   367→        if isinstance(ds, dict):
   368→            _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   369→
   370→    for entry in state_dict.get("scan_history", []):
   371→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   372→            continue
   373→        _rename_key(entry, "raw_findings", "raw_issues")
   374→        for ds in (entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).values():
   375→            if isinstance(ds, dict):
   376→                _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   377→
   378→
   379→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   380→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   381→    migrate_state_keys(state)
   382→
   383→    mutable_state = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   384→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   385→        mutable_state.setdefault(key, value)
   386→
   387→    if not isinstance(state.get("issues"), dict):
   388→        state["issues"] = {}
   389→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   390→        state["stats"] = {}
   391→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   392→        state["scan_history"] = []
   393→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   394→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   395→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   396→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   397→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   398→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   399→
   400→    all_issues = state["issues"]
   401→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   402→    for issue_id, issue in all_issues.items():
   403→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   404→            to_remove.append(issue_id)
   405→            continue
   406→
   407→        issue.setdefault("id", issue_id)
   408→        issue.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   409→        issue.setdefault("file", "")
   410→        issue.setdefault("tier", 3)
   411→        issue.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   412→        issue.setdefault("summary", "")
   413→        issue.setdefault("detail", {})
   414→        issue.setdefault("status", "open")
   415→        issue["status"] = canonical_issue_status(
   416→            issue.get("status"),
   417→            default="open",
   418→        )
   419→        issue.setdefault("note", None)
   420→        issue.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   421→        issue.setdefault("last_seen", issue["first_seen"])
   422→        issue.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   423→        issue["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   424→            issue.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   425→        )
   426→        issue.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   427→        issue.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   428→        issue.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   429→
   430→    for issue_id in to_remove:
   431→        all_issues.pop(issue_id, None)
   432→
   433→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   434→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   435→            continue
   436→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   437→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   438→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   439→
   440→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   441→    return None
   442→
   443→
   444→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   445→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   446→    if not isinstance(state.get("issues"), dict):
   447→        raise ValueError("state.issues must be a dict")
   448→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   449→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   450→
   451→    all_issues = state["issues"]
   452→    for issue_id, issue in all_issues.items():
   453→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   454→            raise ValueError(f"issue {issue_id!r} must be a dict")
   455→        if issue.get("id") != issue_id:
   456→            raise ValueError(f"issue id mismatch for {issue_id!r}")
   457→        if issue.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_ISSUE_STATUSES:
   458→            raise ValueError(
   459→                f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid status {issue.get('status')!r}"
   460→            )
   461→
   462→        tier = issue.get("tier")
   463→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   464→            raise ValueError(f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   465→
   466→        reopen_count = issue.get("reopen_count")
   467→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   468→            raise ValueError(
   469→                f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   470→            )
   471→

     2→

    34→
    35→def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    37→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    38→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    39→    return data
    40→
    41→
    42→def _normalize_loaded_state(data: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    43→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    44→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    45→    ensure_state_defaults(data)
    46→    normalized = cast(StateModel, data)
    47→    validate_state_invariants(normalized)
    48→    return normalized
    49→
    50→
    51→def load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel:
    52→    """Load state from disk, or return empty state on missing/corruption."""
    53→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    54→    if not state_path.exists():
    55→        return empty_state()
    56→
    57→    try:
    58→        data = _load_json(state_path)
    59→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as ex:
    60→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    61→        if backup.exists():
    62→            logger.warning(
    63→                "Primary state load failed for %s; attempting backup %s: %s",
    64→                state_path,
    65→                backup,
    66→                ex,
    67→            )
    68→            try:
    69→                backup_data = _load_json(backup)
    70→                logger.warning(
    71→                    "Recovered state from backup %s after primary load failure at %s",
    72→                    backup,
    73→                    state_path,
    74→                )
    75→                print(
    76→                    f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.",
    77→                    file=sys.stderr,
    78→                )
    79→                return _normalize_loaded_state(backup_data)
    80→            except (
    81→                json.JSONDecodeError,
    82→                UnicodeDecodeError,
    83→                OSError,
    84→                ValueError,
    85→                TypeError,
    86→                AttributeError,
    87→            ) as backup_ex:
    88→                logger.warning(
    89→                    "Backup state load failed from %s after corruption in %s: %s",
    90→                    backup,
    91→                    state_path,
    92→                    backup_ex,
    93→                )
    94→                logger.debug("Backup state load failed from %s: %s", backup, backup_ex)
    95→
    96→        logger.warning(
    97→            "State file load failed for %s and backup recovery was unavailable. "
    98→            "Falling back to empty state: %s",
    99→            state_path,
   100→            ex,
   101→        )
   102→        print(f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   103→        rename_failed = False
   104→        try:
   105→            state_path.rename(state_path.with_suffix(".json.corrupted"))
   106→        except OSError as rename_ex:
   107→            rename_failed = True
   108→            logger.debug(
   109→                "Failed to rename corrupted state file %s: %s", state_path, rename_ex
   110→            )
   111→        if rename_failed:
   112→            logger.debug(
   113→                "Corrupted state file retained at original path: %s", state_path
   114→            )
   115→        return empty_state()
   116→
   117→    version = data.get("version", 1)
   118→    if version > CURRENT_VERSION:
   119→        print(
   120→            "  ⚠ State file version "
   121→            f"{version} is newer than supported ({CURRENT_VERSION}). "
   122→            "Some features may not work correctly.",
   123→            file=sys.stderr,
   124→        )
   125→
   126→    try:
   127→        return _normalize_loaded_state(data)
   128→    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as normalize_ex:
   129→        logger.warning(
   130→            "State invariants invalid for %s; falling back to empty state: %s",
   131→            state_path,
   132→            normalize_ex,
   133→        )
   134→        print(
   135→            f"  ⚠ State invariants invalid ({normalize_ex}). Starting fresh.",
   136→            file=sys.stderr,
   137→        )
   138→        return empty_state()
   139→
   140→
   141→def _coerce_integrity_target(value: object) -> float | None:
   142→    if not is_numeric(value):
   143→        return None
   144→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _resolve_integrity_target(
   148→    state: StateModel,
   149→    explicit_target: float | None,
   150→) -> float | None:
   151→    target = _coerce_integrity_target(explicit_target)
   152→    if target is not None:
   153→        return target
   154→
   155→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   156→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   157→        return None
   158→    return _coerce_integrity_target(integrity.get("target_score"))
   159→
   160→
   161→def save_state(
   162→    state: StateModel,
   163→    path: Path | None = None,
   164→    *,
   165→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   166→) -> None:
   167→    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
   168→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   169→    _recompute_stats(
   170→        state,
   171→        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   172→        subjective_integrity_target=_resolve_integrity_target(
   173→            state,
   174→            subjective_integrity_target,
   175→        ),
   176→    )
   177→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   178→
   179→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
   180→    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   181→
   182→    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   183→
   184→    if state_path.exists():
   185→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   186→        try:
   187→            shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))
   188→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   189→            logger.debug(
   190→                "Failed to create state backup %s: %s",
   191→                state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak"),
   192→                backup_ex,
   193→            )
   194→
   195→    try:
   196→        safe_write_text(state_path, content)
   197→    except OSError as ex:
   198→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save state: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   199→        raise

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    14→    PLAN_VERSION,
    15→    PlanModel,
    16→    empty_plan,
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    18→    validate_plan,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default, utc_now
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    25→
    26→
    27→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    28→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    29→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    30→    if not plan_path.exists():
    31→        return empty_plan()
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    35→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    36→        # Try backup before giving up
    37→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    38→        if backup.exists():
    39→            try:
    40→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    41→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    42→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    43→                # Fall through to validation below
    44→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    45→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
    46→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    47→                return empty_plan()
    48→        else:
    49→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    50→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    51→            return empty_plan()
    52→
    53→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    54→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
    55→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    56→        return empty_plan()
    57→
    58→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    59→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    60→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
    61→        print(
    62→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    63→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→
    67→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    68→    try:
    69→        validate_plan(data)
    70→    except ValueError as ex:
    71→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    72→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    73→        return empty_plan()
    74→
    75→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    76→
    77→
    78→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    80→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    81→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    82→    validate_plan(plan)
    83→
    84→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    85→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    86→
    87→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    88→
    89→    if plan_path.exists():
    90→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    91→        try:
    92→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    93→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    94→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "create plan backup", backup_ex)
    95→
    96→    try:
    97→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    98→    except OSError as ex:
    99→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   100→        raise
   101→
   102→
   103→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   104→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   105→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   106→
   107→
   108→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   109→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   110→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
   111→    if not plan_path.exists():
   112→        return False
   113→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   114→    return bool(
   115→        plan.get("queue_order")
   116→        or plan.get("overrides")
   117→        or plan.get("clusters")
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "PLAN_FILE",
   123→    "has_living_plan",
   124→    "load_plan",
   125→    "plan_path_for_state",
   126→    "save_plan",
   127→]
   128→

    46→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    48→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    49→    state = runtime.state
    50→
    51→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    52→        return
    53→
    54→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    55→    if config_warning:
    56→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    57→
    58→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    59→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    60→
    61→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    62→    if output:
    63→        try:
    64→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    65→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    66→            print_agent_plan(
    67→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    68→                next_command=next_command,
    69→            )
    70→        except OSError as e:
    71→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    72→    else:
    73→        print(plan_md)
    74→        print()
    75→        print_agent_plan(
    76→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    77→            next_command=next_command,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    82→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    83→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    88→    plan = load_plan()
    89→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

    97→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    98→        queue_line = f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
    99→
   100→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})

145→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   146→    version: Required[int]
   147→    created: Required[str]
   148→    updated: Required[str]
   149→    queue_order: list[str]
   150→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   152→    active_cluster: str | None
   153→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   154→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   155→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   156→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   157→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   158→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   159→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   160→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   161→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   162→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   163→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   164→
   165→
   166→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   167→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   168→    now = utc_now()
   169→    return {
   170→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   171→        "created": now,
   172→        "updated": now,
   173→        "queue_order": [],
   174→        "deferred": [],
   175→        "skipped": {},
   176→        "active_cluster": None,
   177→        "overrides": {},
   178→        "clusters": {},
   179→        "superseded": {},
   180→        "promoted_ids": [],
   181→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   182→        "execution_log": [],
   183→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   184→        "commit_log": [],
   185→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   186→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   191→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   192→
   193→    Runtime contract is v7-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   194→    """
   195→    defaults = empty_plan()
   196→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   197→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   198→    _upgrade_plan_to_v7(plan)
   199→
   200→
   201→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   202→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   203→    return {
   204→        name: cluster
   205→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   206→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   212→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   213→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   214→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   215→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   216→
   217→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   218→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   219→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   220→    if overlap:
   221→        raise ValueError(
   222→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   226→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   227→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   228→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   229→            raise ValueError(
   230→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   231→            )
   232→
   233→
   234→__all__ = [
   235→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   236→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   237→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   238→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   239→    "Cluster",
   240→    "CommitRecord",
   241→    "ItemOverride",
   242→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   243→    "PlanModel",
   244→    "PlanStartScores",
   245→    "SkipEntry",
   246→    "SupersededEntry",
   247→    "TriageStagePayload",
   248→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   249→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   250→    "empty_plan",
   251→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   252→    "triage_clusters",
   253→    "validate_plan",
   254→]
   255→

## State and Plan File I/O - Complete Reference

### 1. **State File Path Determination**

The state path is resolved by `state_path()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/state.py`:

**State file naming convention:**
```
.desloppify/state-{lang_name}.json
```

**Resolution logic (in order):**
1. **Explicit `--state` argument** (if provided, uses that exact path)
2. **Language-based path** (primary):
   - Takes the `--lang` argument or auto-detects language
   - Resolves to: `<PROJECT_ROOT>/.desloppify/state-{lang_name}.json`
   - Example: `.desloppify/state-python.json`, `.desloppify/state-typescript.json`
3. **Fallback** (for non-scan commands only):
   - If only one `state-*.json` file exists, use it
   - Scan command always respects language mapping (no fallback)

**Key constants:**
- `STATE_DIR = get_project_root() / ".desloppify"` (from `engine/_state/schema.py`)
- `CURRENT_VERSION = 1` (schema version)

---

### 2. **Plan File Path**

**Plan file location:**
```
.desloppify/plan.json
```

**Derived from state path:**
```python
plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path
# Returns: state_path.parent / "plan.json"
```

**Constants:**
- `PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"` (from `engine/_plan/persistence.py`)
- `PLAN_VERSION = 7` (schema version)

**Loading:**
- `load_plan()` with no arguments loads from `.desloppify/plan.json` by default
- `load_plan(path)` loads from explicit path
- Returns `empty_plan()` if file doesn't exist or is corrupted

---

### 3. **State JSON Schema - Minimum Viable Structure**

**Top-level required keys** (from `engine/_state/schema.py`):

```python
{
    "version": 1,                      # Required[int]
    "created": "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00",  # Required[str] — ISO timestamp
    "last_scan": None,                 # Required[str | None]
    "scan_count": 0,                   # Required[int]
    "overall_score": 0,                # Required[float]
    "objective_score": 0,              # Required[float]
    "strict_score": 0,                 # Required[float]
    "verified_strict_score": 0,        # Required[float]
    "stats": {},                       # Required[dict] — StateStats type
    "issues": {},                      # Required[dict[str, Issue]]
    "subjective_integrity": {},        # Required[SubjectiveIntegrity]
    "subjective_assessments": {},      # Required[dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment]]
    
    # Optional but auto-defaulted:
    "scan_coverage": {},
    "score_confidence": {},
    "lang": None,
    "scan_path": None,
    # ... (many others, all auto-filled by ensure_state_defaults)
}
```

**Minimum viable state** (using `empty_state()` function):
```python
from desloppify.state import empty_state
state = empty_state()
# Already has all required keys populated with sensible defaults
```

**Issue structure** (if you add custom issues):
```python
{
    "issue_id": {
        "id": "issue_id",
        "detector": "detector_name",
        "file": "src/path/to/file.py",
        "tier": 1,                  # int: 1-4
        "confidence": "high",       # str: "high", "medium", "low"
        "summary": "Issue description",
        "detail": {},               # dict[str, Any] — detector-specific data
        "status": "open",           # Status enum: "open", "fixed", "resolved", etc.
        "note": None,               # str | None
        "first_seen": "2026-03-04T00:00:00+00:00",  # ISO timestamp
        "last_seen": "2026-03-04T00:00:00+00:00",   # ISO timestamp
        "resolved_at": None,        # str | None
        "reopen_count": 0,          # int
        # Optional:
        "lang": "python",
        "zone": "production",
    }
}
```

**Top-level required keys** (from `engine/_plan/schema.py`):

```python
{
    "version": 7,                      # Required[int]
    "created": "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00",  # Required[str] — ISO timestamp
    "updated": "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00",  # Required[str] — ISO timestamp
    
    # All auto-defaulted to empty by ensure_plan_defaults():
    "queue_order": [],                 # list[str] — issue IDs in priority order
    "deferred": [],                    # list[str]
    "skipped": {},                     # dict[str, SkipEntry]
    "active_cluster": None,            # str | None
    "overrides": {},                   # dict[str, ItemOverride]
    "clusters": {},                    # dict[str, Cluster]
    "superseded": {},                  # dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    "promoted_ids": [],                # list[str]
    "plan_start_scores": {},           # dict
    "execution_log": [],               # list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    "epic_triage_meta": {},            # dict
    "commit_log": [],                  # list[CommitRecord]
    "uncommitted_issues": [],          # list[str]
    "commit_tracking_branch": None,    # str | None
}
```

**Minimum viable plan** (using `empty_plan()` function):
```python
from desloppify.engine.plan import empty_plan
plan = empty_plan()
# Already has all required keys populated
```

### 5. **How `next` and `plan` Commands Load State/Plan**

**Both commands:**
- Do NOT require `--path` argument
- Auto-resolve state path using `state_path(args)` in `cli.py`
- Auto-resolve plan path as `.desloppify/plan.json`

**Load sequence:**
```python
# From cli.py main():
_load_shared_runtime(args)  # Called once

# Inside _load_shared_runtime():
state_file = state_path(args)  # → Path | None
state = load_state(state_file)  # load from state_file or use default
# state_file can be None (no language detected)
# load_state(None) returns empty_state()

# Later in command handler:
plan = load_plan()  # No args needed
# Defaults to PLAN_FILE = .desloppify/plan.json
```

**Both commands require completed scan:**
```python
require_completed_scan(state)  # Checks state.get("last_scan")
```

---

### 6. **How `scan --path` Determines `.desloppify` Directory**

**Critical detail:** The `--path` argument to `scan` is the **source code directory**, NOT the state directory.

**`.desloppify` location is always:**
```
<PROJECT_ROOT>/.desloppify/
```

**Where `PROJECT_ROOT` is:**
- From `get_project_root()` in `base/discovery/paths.py`
- First directory containing `.git/` going upward from CWD
- Falls back to CWD if no `.git/` found

**Example:**
```bash
# Regardless of where you run this:
desloppify --lang python scan --path ./src

# State is ALWAYS saved to:
<PROJECT_ROOT>/.desloppify/state-python.json
```

---

### 7. **Save/Load Functions**

**State persistence:**
```python
# From engine/_state/persistence.py
load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel
    # If path is None, defaults to STATE_FILE = .desloppify/state.json
    # Returns empty_state() if file missing or corrupted
    # Tries backup (.json.bak) if primary corrupted

save_state(
    state: StateModel,
    path: Path | None = None,
    *,
    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
) -> None
    # If path is None, defaults to STATE_FILE
    # Atomically writes via safe_write_text()
    # Creates backup .json.bak before overwriting
    # Recomputes stats before saving
```

**Plan persistence:**
```python
# From engine/_plan/persistence.py
load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel
    # If path is None, defaults to PLAN_FILE = .desloppify/plan.json
    # Returns empty_plan() if missing or corrupted
    # Tries backup (.json.bak) if primary corrupted

save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None
    # If path is None, defaults to PLAN_FILE
    # Atomically writes via safe_write_text()
    # Creates backup .json.bak before overwriting
    # Auto-updates "updated" timestamp
```

---

### 8. **Safe File Writing Pattern**

Used by both state and plan persistence:

```python
# From base/discovery/file_paths.py
def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    """Atomically write text using temp+rename."""
    p = Path(filepath)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
            f.write(content)
        os.replace(tmp, str(p))  # Atomic on POSIX
    except OSError:
        if os.path.exists(tmp):
            os.unlink(tmp)
        raise
```

### 9. **Script Example: Creating Spoofed State Files**

```python
from pathlib import Path
import json
from desloppify.state import empty_state, save_state
from desloppify.engine.plan import empty_plan, save_plan

# Get project root (wherever .git/ is)
project_root = Path([REDACTED])
state_dir = project_root / ".desloppify"

# Create Python state file
state = empty_state()
state["lang"] = "python"
state["scan_path"] = "desloppify"  # Relative to project root
state["scan_count"] = 1
state["last_scan"] = "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00"

# Optionally add an issue
state["issues"]["smells::desloppify/cli.py::test"] = {
    "id": "smells::desloppify/cli.py::test",
    "detector": "smells",
    "file": "desloppify/cli.py",
    "tier": 2,
    "confidence": "high",
    "summary": "Test issue",
    "detail": {},
    "status": "open",
    "note": None,
    "first_seen": "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00",
    "last_seen": "2026-03-04T12:00:00+00:00",
    "resolved_at": None,
    "reopen_count": 0,
}

# Save atomically
state_path = state_dir / "state-python.json"
save_state(state, state_path)

# Create empty plan (optional)
plan = empty_plan()
plan_path = state_dir / "plan.json"
save_plan(plan, plan_path)
```

### **Summary Table**

| Item | Path | Notes |
|------|------|-------|
| **State directory** | `.desloppify/` | Always relative to `PROJECT_ROOT` |
| **State file (Python)** | `.desloppify/state-python.json` | Auto-resolved by language |
| **State file (TypeScript)** | `.desloppify/state-typescript.json` | Auto-resolved by language |
| **Plan file** | `.desloppify/plan.json` | One plan per project |
| **State schema version** | `1` | From `CURRENT_VERSION` |
| **Plan schema version** | `7` | From `PLAN_VERSION` |
| **Backup files** | `.json.bak` | Created before overwrite |
| **Corrupted files** | `.json.corrupted` | Renamed on load failure |
